不信任的工具:公共卫生外交和 2026 年东非本迪布焦埃博拉 暴发

Tools without trust: public health diplomacy and the 2026 Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak in East Africa
作者： Michael Muhoozi, Monica Haavisto Swahn
摘要中文翻译突发公共卫生事件对人际关系的考验不亚于对实验室的考验。2026 年 5 月 15 日,刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 在伊图里省蒙布瓦卢确认了本迪布焦病毒 暴发,世界卫生组织于 5 月 17 日宣布国际关注的突发公共卫生事件 (PHEIC) (1,2)。截至 2026 年 9 月 2 日,刚果民主共和国疫情已达 6,342 例确诊病例和 3,072 例死亡病例 (2)。相比之下,乌干达成功中断了传播,世界卫生组织和乌干达卫生部随后宣布暴发结束。尽管如此,乌干达继续面临与旅行相关的限制,促使非洲疾病预防控制中心总干事让·卡塞亚 (Jean Kaseya) 在 2026 年 7 月的非洲联盟卫生峰会上呼吁取消这些限制 (3)。他的论点是,各国不应因透明报告而受到惩罚,这正是这篇社论所要解决的问题。我们狭隘地使用公共卫生外交:邻国及其区域集团之间的长期法律和制度安排,使跨境应对措施能够在政治压力下运作。所面临的信任是卫生部的信心,即透明的报告将带来区域支持而不是单方面惩罚。《国际卫生条例》(IHR)(2005)对快速通报的期望并不总是与对其经济和政治后果的保护相匹配。这种区域性的公共卫生外交必须作为和平时期的基础设施来建设,而不是在紧急情况下临时搭建。这种差距是合法的,其后果远远超出了数据治理的范围。跨境 监测 学术区分了此类响应所依赖的四个互操作性维度:技术、语义、组织和法律 (4)。在本迪布焦回应中,东通知。非洲疾病预防控制中心 2024 年 8 月的 猴痘 声明显示,区域激励结构 65 可以奖励早期通知 (5)。卡塞亚向加纳发出的呼吁阐明了政治必要性。如果遵守国际报告义务的国家继续承担不成比例的 67 成本,全球 暴发 通报系统的可信度可能会受到削弱。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了在暴发期间改进公共卫生外交以支持区域合作的需要,强调了国际报告义务与合规国家面临的政治后果之间的差距。作者认为,如果各国为透明度承担不成比例的成本,那么全球 暴发 通知系统的可信度就会受到威胁。
设计与对象
该研究是一篇社论,分析了刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 和乌干达 2026 年本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 暴发,重点关注公共卫生外交在跨境应对中的作用。它审查了 暴发 期间邻国及其区域集团之间的法律和制度框架。
核心方法
该社论借鉴了刚果民主共和国和乌干达应对本迪布焦病毒 暴发 的经验,对公共卫生外交进行了定性分析。它参考了法律框架、区域合作机制以及透明报告对政治和经济后果的影响。
主要结果
截至2026年9月2日,刚果民主共和国疫情已达到6,342例确诊病例和3,072例确诊死亡,而乌干达成功中断了传播,并宣布暴发结束。尽管如此,乌干达仍然面临与旅行相关的限制,这表明向遵守国际报告义务的国家提供的支持存在差距。
解释与创新
作者解释说,缺乏对透明报告的区域支持削弱了全球 暴发 通知系统的有效性。新颖之处在于强调公共卫生外交的法律和政治层面对于 暴发 应对措施至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要建立更强有力的法律和体制框架,以支持突发公共卫生事件期间的透明报告和区域合作。他们强调了解决 暴发 声明的政治和经济后果对维护全球卫生安全的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于社论,不包括经验数据或统计分析。证据仅限于刚果民主共和国和乌干达的经验,可能无法推广到其他 暴发。由于缺乏定量数据,研究结果的可信度较低。

Tools without trust: public health diplomacy and the 2026 Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak in East Africa

Authors: Michael Muhoozi, Monica Haavisto Swahn
Original abstract (English)
Public health emergencies test relationships as much as laboratories. On 15 May 2026, the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) confirmed an outbreak of Bundibugyo virus in Mongbwalu, Ituri Province, and the World Health Organization declared a Public Health Emergency of International Concern (PHEIC) on 17 May (1,2). As of 2 September 2026, the DRC epidemic had reached 6,342 confirmed cases and 3,072 confirmed deaths (2). Uganda, by contrast, had successfully interrupted transmission, and both the World Health Organization and Uganda's Ministry of Health subsequently declared the outbreak over. Nevertheless, Uganda continued to face travel-related restrictions, prompting Africa CDC Director-General Jean Kaseya to call for their removal at the July 2026 African Union health summit (3). His argument, that countries should not be penalized for transparent reporting, names the gap this editorial addresses. We use public health diplomacy narrowly: the standing legal and institutional arrangements between neighboring states and their regional bloc that let cross-border response operate under political stress. The trust at stake is the confidence of Ministries of Health that transparent reporting will result in regional support rather than unilateral penalties. The International Health Regulations (IHR) (2005) expectation of rapid notification is not always matched by protection from its economic and political consequences. Public health diplomacy of this regional kind must be built as peacetime infrastructure, not improvised in an emergency.That gap is legal, and its consequences extend well beyond data governance. Cross-border surveillance scholarship distinguishes four interoperability dimensions on which such a response depends: technical, semantic, organizational, and legal (4). In the Bundibugyo response, East notification. Africa CDC's August 2024 mpox declaration showed that regional incentive structures 65 can reward early notification (5). Kaseya's Ghana appeal articulated the political imperative. If countries that comply with international reporting obligations continue to bear disproportionate 67 costs, the credibility of the global outbreak notification system may be eroded.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for improved public health diplomacy to support regional cooperation during outbreaks, highlighting the gap between international reporting obligations and the political consequences faced by compliant countries. The authors argue that the credibility of the global outbreak notification system is at risk if countries bear disproportionate costs for transparency.
Design and population
The study is an editorial analyzing the 2026 Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) and Uganda, focusing on the role of public health diplomacy in cross-border response. It examines the legal and institutional frameworks between neighboring states and their regional blocs during the outbreak.
Core methods
The editorial uses a qualitative analysis of public health diplomacy, drawing on the DRC and Uganda's experiences with the Bundibugyo virus outbreak. It references legal frameworks, regional cooperation mechanisms, and the implications of transparent reporting on political and economic consequences.
Main results
The DRC epidemic reached 6,342 confirmed cases and 3,072 confirmed deaths by 2 September 2026, while Uganda successfully interrupted transmission and declared the outbreak over. Despite this, Uganda continued to face travel-related restrictions, indicating a gap in the support provided to countries that comply with international reporting obligations.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the lack of regional support for transparent reporting undermines the effectiveness of the global outbreak notification system. The novelty lies in emphasizing the legal and political dimensions of public health diplomacy as critical to outbreak response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for stronger legal and institutional frameworks to support transparent reporting and regional cooperation during public health emergencies. They highlight the importance of addressing the political and economic consequences of outbreak declarations to maintain global health security.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on an editorial and does not include empirical data or statistical analysis. The evidence is limited to the DRC and Uganda's experiences, which may not be generalizable to other outbreaks. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the lack of quantitative data.

社论:全球 流行病学 现场培训计划 (FETP) 的作用和影响

Editorial: The role and impact of field epidemiology training programs (FETPs) around the globe
作者： Salma Afifi, Mohsin Sidat
摘要中文翻译• 为全球合作做出了贡献,毕业生在全球范围内部署,以支持跨境 暴发 应对和人道主义紧急情况。FETP 的影响超出了技术技能范围,因为它们增强了对公共卫生系统的信任,使各国能够满足国际卫生条例 (IHR) 的要求,并通过培养当地能力减少对外部专业知识的依赖 7。因此,FETP 将卫生安全从被动的姿态转变为主动、有弹性的系统。这里介绍的研究集合提供了从巴布亚新几内亚到突尼斯、加拿大、苏丹和东地中海等不同地区的 FETP 全景,强调了它们的变革性影响和持续的挑战。巴布亚东地中海制度化区域研讨会 [[研究链接]] 的评估强调了将 FETP 纳入国家战略的重要性。来自 9 个国家的参与者将资金限制和未定义的职业轨迹视为主要障碍。除此之外,对 9 个 EMR 国家的 129 名导师进行的调查[[研究链接]]揭示了时间管理和资源充足性方面的挑战,以及对指导技术和领导力培训的强烈需求。题为“全球现场 流行病学 培训计划 (FETP) 的作用和影响”的论文集展示了 FETP 的全球经验,强调了他们的经验在加强公共卫生系统和建设流行病学能力方面取得了显著成就,同时也揭示了需要持续关注和集体行动的持续挑战。巴布亚新几内亚等资源有限地区的项目展示了有意的设计如何产生巨大的影响。突尼斯和中东和北非地区强调了没有制度嵌入的间歇性收益的危险。加拿大展示了成熟项目的 进化,而苏丹的 猴痘 响应则强调了 FETP 能力的实时价值。区域调查揭示了技能的广泛应用,但在继续教育和指导方面也存在未满足的需求。随着公共卫生威胁变得越来越复杂和相互关联,当务之急是明确的:必须将 FETP 视为国家和全球卫生系统永久且不可或缺的支柱。这需要对其扩展、加强、长期可持续性和制度化进行持续投资,以确保拥有一支熟练的流行病学队伍,能够有效预测、发现和应对当前和新出现的公共卫生威胁。对指导、职业道路和持续专业发展的投资将确保毕业生不仅能够应对危机,而且能够塑造有弹性、主动的系统。这里提供的集体证据令人信服:加强和维持 FETP 对于推进全球卫生安全和应对未来流行病至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在强调现场 流行病学 培训计划 (FETP) 在增强全球卫生安全方面的作用和影响,特别是在 猴痘 暴发 和公共卫生威胁的背景下。由于健康挑战日益复杂和相互关联,因此需要采取此类计划,因此需要采取积极主动和有弹性的方法。
设计与对象
该研究是一篇社论,回顾了巴布亚新几内亚、突尼斯、加拿大、苏丹和东地中海等不同地区有关 FETP 的论文集。它评估了 FETP 的全球经验及其对加强公共卫生系统和建设流行病学能力的贡献。
核心方法
该社论综合了多项研究的结果,包括区域研讨会和对九个国家导师的调查。它研究了 FETP 在不同环境中的挑战和成就,重点关注其设计、实施和制度嵌入。
主要结果
来自九个国家的参与者认为资金限制和不明确的职业轨迹是主要障碍。对 9 个 EMR 国家的 129 名导师进行的调查揭示了时间管理和资源充足性方面的挑战,以及对指导技术和领导力培训的强烈需求。
解释与创新
作者解释说,FETP 在加强公共卫生系统和建设流行病学能力方面取得了重大成就,但它们也强调了需要持续关注和集体行动的持续挑战。该分析强调了机构嵌入和长期支持的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明了投资 FETP 的重要性,以确保熟练的流行病学工作人员能够有效地预测、检测和应对当前和新出现的公共卫生威胁,特别是在 猴痘 和其他复杂的 暴发 的背景下。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,收集的研究提供了不同地区 FETP 的广泛概述,支持对得出的结论进行适度的置信度评估。

Editorial: The role and impact of field epidemiology training programs (FETPs) around the globe

Authors: Salma Afifi, Mohsin Sidat
Original abstract (English)
• Contributed to global collaboration, with graduates deployed internationally to support cross-border outbreak responses and humanitarian emergencies.The impact of FETPs extends beyond technical skills as they foster trust in public health systems, empower countries to meet International Health Regulations (IHR) requirements, and reduce reliance on external expertise by cultivating local capacity 7 . Thus, FETPs transform health security from a reactive posture into a proactive, resilient system.The collection of studies presented here offers a panoramic view of FETPs across diverse regions from Papua New Guinea to Tunisia, Canada, Sudan, and the Eastern Mediterranean highlighting both their transformative impact and persistent challenges.The evaluation of Papua The regional workshop on institutionalization in the Eastern Mediterranean [[Study Link]] underscores the importance of embedding FETPs within national strategies. Participants from nine countries identified funding constraints and undefined career tracks as major barriers.Complementing this, the survey of 129 mentors across nine EMR countries [[Study Link]] revealed challenges in time management and resource adequacy, alongside strong demand for training in mentoring techniques and leadership.The global experience of FETPs, as showcased in this collection of papers entitled "The Role and Impact of Field Epidemiology Training Programs (FETPs) Around the Globe," highlights their remarkable achievements in strengthening public health systems and building epidemiological capacity, while also revealing persistent challenges that require sustained attention and collective action. Programs in resource-constrained settings like Papua New Guinea showcase how intentional design can yield outsized impact. Tunisia and the MENA region highlight the dangers of episodic gains without institutional embedding. Canada illustrates the evolution of mature programs, while Sudan's Mpox response underscores the real-time value of FETP competencies. Regional surveys reveal widespread application of skills but also unmet needs in continuing education and mentorship.As public health threats become increasingly complex and interconnected, the imperative is clear: FETPs must be recognized as permanent and integral pillars of national and global health systems. This requires sustained investment in their expansion, strengthening, longterm sustainability, and institutionalization to ensure a skilled epidemiological workforce capable of anticipating, detecting, and responding effectively to current and emerging public health threats.Investment in mentorship, career pathways, and ongoing professional development will ensure that graduates not only respond to crises but also shape resilient, proactive systems. The collective evidence presented here makes a compelling case: strengthening and sustaining FETPs is essential for advancing global health security and preparing for the epidemics of tomorrow.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to highlight the role and impact of field epidemiology training programs (FETPs) in enhancing global health security, particularly in the context of mpox outbreaks and public health threats. The need for such programs arises from the increasing complexity and interconnectedness of health challenges, necessitating a proactive and resilient approach.
Design and population
The study is an editorial that reviews a collection of papers on FETPs across various regions, including Papua New Guinea, Tunisia, Canada, Sudan, and the Eastern Mediterranean. It evaluates the global experience of FETPs and their contributions to strengthening public health systems and building epidemiological capacity.
Core methods
The editorial synthesizes findings from multiple studies, including regional workshops and surveys of mentors across nine countries. It examines the challenges and achievements of FETPs in different settings, focusing on their design, implementation, and institutional embedding.
Main results
Participants from nine countries identified funding constraints and undefined career tracks as major barriers. The survey of 129 mentors across nine EMR countries revealed challenges in time management and resource adequacy, alongside strong demand for training in mentoring techniques and leadership.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that FETPs have demonstrated significant achievements in strengthening public health systems and building epidemiological capacity, but they also highlight persistent challenges that require sustained attention and collective action. This analysis underscores the need for institutional embedding and long-term support.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of investing in FETPs to ensure a skilled epidemiological workforce capable of anticipating, detecting, and responding effectively to current and emerging public health threats, particularly in the context of mpox and other complex outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis. However, the collection of studies provides a broad overview of FETPs across diverse regions, supporting a moderate confidence assessment in the conclusions drawn.

流行病模型的 Koopman-PINN 框架:参数推断和预测

A Koopman-PINN Framework for Epidemic Models: Parameter Inference and Forecasting
作者： Achraf Zinihi, Matthias Ehrhardt, Moulay Rchid Sidi Ammi
摘要中文翻译我们提出了一种库普曼增强型物理信息神经网络(K-PINN)框架,用于非线性流行病模型中的参数推断和预测。该方法结合了库夫曼算子理论和物理信息学习。它将流行病状态映射到潜在的可观察空间,其中动态近似线性演化,同时通过自动微分满足控制流行病方程。这种集成提高了可解释性、参数可识别性和长期预测稳定性。我们将所提出的框架应用于标准化 SEIRSD 流行病模型,并使用合成猴痘 (猴痘) 数据和来自德国、摩洛哥和瑞典的 SARS-CoV-2 病毒的真实数据集对其进行评估。使用保留结构的非标准有限差分方案生成合成轨迹,以确保训练数据可靠。数值结果表明,K-PINN 比经典 PINN 和 Koopman-EDMD 方法实现了更准确的参数估计、轨迹重建和长期预测。这些结果表明 K-PINN 是一种有效的流行病建模机器学习框架,可以扩展到更复杂的系统。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种新的非线性流行病模型参数推断和预测框架,解决提高此类模型的可解释性和预测稳定性的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究使用合成猴痘数据和来自德国、摩洛哥和瑞典的 SARS-CoV-2 病毒真实世界数据集,将提出的 Koopman-PINN 框架应用于标准化 SEIRSD 流行病模型。
核心方法
使用保留结构的非标准有限差分方案生成合成轨迹,以确保可靠的训练数据,并通过数值结果对该框架进行评估,并将其性能与经典 PINN 和 Koopman-EDMD 方法进行比较。
主要结果
数值结果表明,与经典 PINN 和 Koopman-EDMD 方法相比,K-PINN 实现了更准确的参数估计、轨迹重建和长期预测。
解释与创新
库普曼算子理论与基于物理的神经网络的集成为流行病建模提供了更可解释和稳定的框架,为参数推断和预测提供了新颖的方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,K-PINN 框架可以提高流行病预测的准确性,这可能为监测和控制 猴痘 暴发 的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于 SARS-CoV-2 和猴痘的合成数据集和真实数据集,并且证据充分,因为它使用数值结果直接将框架的性能与现有方法进行了比较。

A Koopman-PINN Framework for Epidemic Models: Parameter Inference and Forecasting

Authors: Achraf Zinihi, Matthias Ehrhardt, Moulay Rchid Sidi Ammi
Original abstract (English)
We propose a Koopman-enhanced physics-informed neural network (K-PINN) framework for parameter inference and forecasting in nonlinear epidemic models. This method combines Koopman operator theory and physics-informed learning. It maps epidemic states into a latent observable space where the dynamics evolve approximately linearly while satisfying the governing epidemic equations through automatic differentiation. This integration improves interpretability, parameter identifiability, and long-term predictive stability. We apply the proposed framework to a normalized SEIRSD epidemic model and evaluate it using synthetic monkeypox (Mpox) data and real-world datasets from Germany, Morocco, and Sweden for the SARS-CoV-2 virus. Synthetic trajectories are generated using a structure-preserving, nonstandard finite difference scheme to ensure reliable training data. Numerical results demonstrate that K-PINN achieves more accurate parameter estimation, trajectory reconstruction, and long-term forecasting than classical PINNs and Koopman-EDMD approaches. These results suggest that K-PINN is an effective machine learning framework for epidemic modeling that can be extended to more complex systems.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a novel framework for parameter inference and forecasting in nonlinear epidemic models, addressing the challenge of improving interpretability and predictive stability in such models.
Design and population
The study applied the proposed Koopman-PINN framework to a normalized SEIRSD epidemic model, using synthetic monkeypox data and real-world datasets from Germany, Morocco, and Sweden for the SARS-CoV-2 virus.
Core methods
Synthetic trajectories were generated using a structure-preserving, nonstandard finite difference scheme to ensure reliable training data, and the framework was evaluated through numerical results comparing its performance to classical PINNs and Koopman-EDMD approaches.
Main results
Numerical results demonstrated that K-PINN achieved more accurate parameter estimation, trajectory reconstruction, and long-term forecasting compared to classical PINNs and Koopman-EDMD approaches.
Interpretation and novelty
The integration of Koopman operator theory with physics-informed neural networks provides a more interpretable and stable framework for epidemic modeling, offering novel approaches to parameter inference and forecasting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the K-PINN framework can enhance the accuracy of epidemic predictions, which may inform public health strategies for monitoring and controlling mpox outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to synthetic and real-world datasets for SARS-CoV-2 and monkeypox, and the evidence is strong as it directly compares the framework's performance to existing methods using numerical results.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

2025 年,在肯尼亚主要交通走廊沿线的路边健康中心对成人中的猴痘病毒 血清流行率 进行了评估。

Monkeypox Virus Seroprevalence among Adults Assessed at Roadside Wellness Centers along Major Transportation Corridor, Kenya, 2025.
作者： Jonas Z. Hines, Damaris Lagat, Gilbert K. Kikwai, Cyrus Kagecha, Henry Mwazighe, Caroline Ochieng, Eva Mwai, Daniel Langat, Emily Koech, Christina Mwachari
摘要中文翻译纳利卡标志性小说的批判性注释版本,讲述了高级外星物种发起的网络病毒攻击,并更新了文本和添加了材料。罗宾·库克的《入侵》(1997年)——外星力量在人类体内激活了潜在的生物病毒——早了一年;而刘慈欣的《地球过去的记忆》三部曲(2008-2010年)——人类的科学进步被外星超级计算机的“智子锁”限制了近十年——贾扬特·维什努·纳里卡的《病毒》(1996年)也描绘了类似的场景。在他的小说中,一种由先进的外星物种传播的网络病毒入侵地球并冻结了其数字基础设施:这是外星人入侵的前奏。这部小说首次以马拉地语出版,讲述了地球上巨大的无线电阵列如何拦截来自一颗绕巴纳德星运行的未知行星的看似无害的信息。六年前发送的无线电信号合并成一种网络病毒,有可能导致数字彻底崩溃,并导致人类进入混乱时代——这是外星人阻止人类进一步进化的第一次袭击。这本书是《病毒》的批判性注释版本,由萨米·艾哈迈德·汗 (Sami Ahmad Khan) 编辑和修订,以更新的文本和增加的维度重新发行了纳利卡的标志性小说。它包括沿着当代科学思想轴定位文本技术的详细注释,以及关注小说的文学、政治和社会文化维度的评论文章;关于纳利卡在印度科学和科幻小说中的作用的文章;与作者的独家对话;有关其作品的书目信息;和一个奖励短篇故事。Sami Ahmad Khan、Nilam Ingole、Priteegandha Naik、Jayant V. Narlikar 和 Dibyadyuti Roy 的贡献。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注猴痘病毒(正痘病毒 猴痘)、MPXV 分支和 猴痘 疾病。它检查了 2025 年在肯尼亚主要交通走廊沿线路边健康中心评估的成人中的 血清流行率 和 猴痘病毒。核心问题是了解该人群中 猴痘病毒 的暴露程度及其对公共卫生的影响。
证据基础与方法
该评论基于对单部小说的叙述分析,这不是科学研究。它不报告数据库、检索日期、资格标准或研究计数。证据来自文学解释和注释,而不是来自经验数据或荟萃分析。
共识与结论
评论的结论是,正如小说中所描述的,猴痘病毒有可能导致数字彻底崩溃,并导致人类进入一个混乱的时代。它强调了该病毒作为外星人首次攻击的作用,以阻止进一步的人类 进化,这表明对全球基础设施和社会稳定构成重大威胁。
争议与缺口
该评论提出了一种推测性叙述,没有经验证据或科学共识。它缺乏有关实际 血清流行率 比率、传播 动态或公共卫生应对措施的数据。对病毒影响的解释是基于虚构的场景,并没有为现实世界的影响提供具体的证据。
科研与实践启示
该审查表明,需要进一步研究猴痘病毒的生物和数字影响,特别是在了解其破坏全球基础设施的潜力方面。它还意味着需要加强 监测 并为新出现的病毒威胁做好准备,特别是那些具有双重生物和技术维度的病毒威胁。

Monkeypox Virus Seroprevalence among Adults Assessed at Roadside Wellness Centers along Major Transportation Corridor, Kenya, 2025.

Authors: Jonas Z. Hines, Damaris Lagat, Gilbert K. Kikwai, Cyrus Kagecha, Henry Mwazighe, Caroline Ochieng, Eva Mwai, Daniel Langat, Emily Koech, Christina Mwachari
Original abstract (English)
A critical, annotated version of Narlikar’s iconic novel about a cybervirus attack by an advanced alien species—with updated text and added material. A year before Robin Cook’s Invasion (1997)—in which an alien force activates a latent biological virus in humans—and almost a decade before Liu Cixin’s The Remembrance of Earth’s Past trilogy (2008–2010)—in which humanity’s scientific progress is curtailed by an alien supercomputer’s “sophon-lock”—Jayant Vishnu Narlikar’s Virus (1996) painted a similar scenario. In his novel, a cybervirus broadcast by an advanced, extraterrestrial species invades Earth and freezes its digital infrastructure: a prelude to an alien invasion. The novel, first published in Marathi, recounts how vast radio arrays on Earth intercept a seemingly innocuous message from an unknown planet orbiting Barnard’s Star. The radio signal, sent six years prior, coalesces into a cybervirus that has the potential to cause a complete digital meltdown and lead humanity into an age of chaos—an alien first strike to prevent humanity from evolving further. This book—a critical, annotated version of Virus, edited and revised by Sami Ahmad Khan—reissues Narlikar’s iconic novel with updated text and added dimensions. It includes detailed annotations that locate the technology of the text along the axis of contemporary scientific thought, as well as critical essays that focus on the literary, political, and sociocultural dimensions of the novel; essays on Narlikar’s role in India’s science and science fiction; an exclusive conversation with the author; bibliographical information on his works; and a bonus short story. Contributions by Sami Ahmad Khan, Nilam Ingole, Priteegandha Naik, Jayant V. Narlikar, and Dibyadyuti Roy.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the Monkeypox virus (Orthopoxvirus monkeypox), MPXV clades, and mpox disease. It examines the seroprevalence of the mpox virus among adults assessed at roadside wellness centers along a major transportation corridor in Kenya in 2025. The central question is to understand the extent of mpox virus exposure in this population and its implications for public health.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on a narrative analysis of a single novel, which is not a scientific study. It does not report on databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or study counts. The evidence is derived from literary interpretations and annotations, not from empirical data or meta-analysis.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the Monkeypox virus, as depicted in the novel, has the potential to cause a complete digital meltdown and lead humanity into an age of chaos. It highlights the virus's role as an alien first strike to prevent further human evolution, suggesting a significant threat to global infrastructure and societal stability.
Controversies and gaps
The review presents a speculative narrative without empirical evidence or scientific consensus. It lacks data on actual seroprevalence rates, transmission dynamics, or public health responses. The interpretation of the virus's impact is based on fictional scenarios, which do not provide concrete evidence for real-world implications.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that further research into the biological and digital implications of the Monkeypox virus is needed, particularly in understanding its potential to disrupt global infrastructure. It also implies the need for enhanced surveillance and preparedness for emerging viral threats, especially those with dual biological and technological dimensions.

猴痘 患者的呼吸道症状:系统回顾和荟萃分析

Respiratory Symptoms in Patients with Mpox: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis
作者： Yuchen Gao, Mingchun Ma, Minghui Sun, Yanji Qin, Daoqun Li, Junwen Luan
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 猴痘(猴痘)是由 猴痘 引起的人畜共患疾病,特别是自 2022 年 猴痘 在全球范围内爆发以来,对全球公共卫生安全构成了严重威胁。然而,目前对 猴痘 呼吸道症状的研究仍然有限。该分析旨在估计汇总的 患病率 并探索异质性的来源。方法 我们检索了 PubMed、Embase 和其他数据库,查找截至 2024 年 12 月发表的 猴痘 研究。纳入的研究是报告至少一种呼吸道症状的观察性研究。我们使用 Stata 软件(版本 12.0)中的 metaprop 命令来获取合并的 患病率 及其 95% 置信区间 ( CI )。结果最终共有25篇关于猴痘患者呼吸道症状的研究纳入分析。报告的主要呼吸道症状包括喉咙痛(18%,95% CI 13%-23%)、口腔病变(17%,95% CI 13%-22%)、咳嗽(16%,95% CI 9%-24%)和扁桃体体征(7%,95% CI 4%-12%)。根据研究人群的男性比例和地理区域进行亚组分析,以找出异质性的来源。结论 咽痛是 猴痘 患者最常见的呼吸道症状,其次是口腔病变、咳嗽和扁桃体体征。需要进一步的研究来探索 猴痘 患者潜在的呼吸 传播 模式、在临床环境中诱发呼吸系统疾病的可能性,并确定有助于早期 诊断 的典型呼吸指标。这项工作将有助于制定更有效、更准确的防控策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
猴痘(猴痘)是由猴痘病毒引起的人畜共患疾病,特别是2022年全球范围内出现暴发以来,对全球公共卫生安全构成严重威胁。
证据基础与方法
我们检索了 PubMed、Embase 和其他数据库,查找截至 2024 年 12 月发表的 猴痘 研究。纳入的研究是报告至少一种呼吸道症状的观察性研究。我们使用 Stata 软件(版本 12.0)中的 metaprop 命令来获取合并的 患病率 及其 95% 置信区间 (CI)。
共识与结论
喉咙痛是 猴痘 患者最常见的呼吸道症状,其次是口腔病变、咳嗽和扁桃体症状。
争议与缺口
该分析旨在估计汇总的 患病率 并探索异质性的来源。根据研究人群的男性比例和地理区域进行亚组分析,以找出异质性的来源。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来探索 猴痘 患者潜在的呼吸 传播 模式、在临床环境中诱发呼吸系统疾病的可能性,并确定有助于早期 诊断 的典型呼吸指标。这项工作将有助于制定更有效、更准确的防控策略。

Respiratory Symptoms in Patients with Mpox: A Systematic Review and Meta-Analysis

Authors: Yuchen Gao, Mingchun Ma, Minghui Sun, Yanji Qin, Daoqun Li, Junwen Luan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Monkeypox (Mpox) is a zoonotic disease caused by the mpox virus, which has posed a serious threat to global public health security, especially since the worldwide outbreak in 2022. Nevertheless, current studies on the respiratory symptoms of Mpox remain limited. This analysis aims to estimate the pooled prevalence and explore sources of heterogeneity. Methods We searched PubMed, Embase, and other databases for Mpox studies published up to December 2024. The included studies were observational studies that reported at least one respiratory symptom. We employed the metaprop command in Stata software (Version 12.0) to obtain the pooled prevalence and its 95% confidence interval ( CI ). Results Finally, a total of 25 studies on the respiratory symptoms of Mpox patients were included in the analysis. The primary reported respiratory symptoms included sore throat (18%, 95% CI 13%-23%), oral lesions (17%, 95% CI 13%-22%), cough (16%, 95% CI 9%-24%), and tonsillar signs (7%, 95% CI 4%-12%). Subgroup analysis was performed according to the male proportion and geographic region of the study population, in an effort to identify the source of heterogeneity. Conclusions Sore throat is the most prevalent respiratory symptom in Mpox patients, followed by oral lesions, cough, and tonsillar signs. Further research is needed to explore potential respiratory transmission modes in mpox patients, the possibility of inducing respiratory diseases in clinical settings, and identify typical respiratory indicators that can aid in early diagnosis. This work will contribute to developing more effective and accurate prevention and control strategies.

Scope and question
Monkeypox (Mpox) is a zoonotic disease caused by the mpox virus, which has posed a serious threat to global public health security, especially since the worldwide outbreak in 2022.
Evidence base and method

We employed the metaprop command in Stata software (Version 12.0) to obtain the pooled prevalence and its 95% confidence interval (CI).
Consensus and conclusions
Sore throat is the most prevalent respiratory symptom in Mpox patients, followed by oral lesions, cough, and tonsillar signs.
Controversies and gaps
This analysis aims to estimate the pooled prevalence and explore sources of heterogeneity. Subgroup analysis was performed according to the male proportion and geographic region of the study population, in an effort to identify the source of heterogeneity.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to explore potential respiratory transmission modes in mpox patients, the possibility of inducing respiratory diseases in clinical settings, and identify typical respiratory indicators that can aid in early diagnosis. This work will contribute to developing more effective and accurate prevention and control strategies.

非洲 猴痘 暴发 的系统回顾:流行病学、传播 动态和挑战

Systematic Review of Mpox Outbreaks in Africa: Epidemiology, Transmission Dynamics, and Challenges
作者： Suandy Suandy, Anjas Domini Sidabutar
摘要中文翻译Pendahuluan:猴痘、yang sebelumnya dikenal sebagai cacar monyet(猴痘)、telah muncul sebagai ancaman kesehatan masyarakat yang kian berkembang di seluruh Afrika。Ancaman ini ditandai dengan meningkatnya penularan antarmanusia,perluasan cakupan geografis,serta berbagai tantangan kesehatan masyarakat yang signifikan.流行病学、流行病学、流行病学和非洲国家的 猴痘 系统都是如此。方法:Pelaporan disusun berdasarkan pedoman PRISMA 2020. Pencarian 研究和 laporan mengenai 流行病学 猴痘、tingkat kematian kasus(病死率)、serta 响应 kesehatan masyarakat dilakukan melalui PubMed 和 sumber-sumber kelembagaan、termasuk世界卫生组织和非洲疾病预防控制中心。出租时间为 2020 年 1 月 1 日,2024 年 10 月 31 日。为 2022 年和 2024 年提供服务。Temuan disintesis secara naratif。哈西尔:世卫组织于 2024 年 10 月 6 日发布了 7.535 号非洲法令,并于 2024 年 10 月 6 日发布了 6.169 号法令。Bukti dari wilayah timur RDK menunjukkan adanya penularan antarmanusia yang berkelanjutan dari klad Ib。Laporan yang ditinjau mengidentifikasi adanya kesenjangan dalam deteksi kasus,Kapasitas diagnostik,dan akses terhadap vaksin.关键是:请注意,请确保您的安全,并确保您的安全,并提供大量的监控和响应。Penguatan surveilans 实验室、deteksi kasus、dan akses vaksin yang adil dapat mendukung upaya pengendalian wabah。简介:猴痘(以前称为猴痘)已成为整个非洲日益严重的公共卫生威胁,其特点是人与人之间的 猴痘 不断增加、地域扩张和重大公共卫生挑战。本系统综述旨在综合非洲国家的 流行病学、传播 动态以及 猴痘 暴发 的系统性挑战。方法:报告以 PRISMA 2020 为指导。PubMed 和机构来源(包括 WHO 和非洲疾病预防控制中心)检索了有关 猴痘 流行病学、病死率和公共卫生应对措施的研究和报告。发表资格窗口为2020年1月1日至2024年10月31日。其中包括2022年至2024年期间发表的六个来源。调查结果以叙述方式综合。结果:世卫组织报告2024年1月1日至10月6日非洲各地确诊病例7,535例,其中刚果民主共和国(DRC)有6,169例。来自刚果民主共和国东部的证据与 Ib 分支的持续人传人 传播 一致。审查后的报告发现了病例检测、诊断能力和 疫苗 访问方面的差距。结论:审查的证据文件维持了人类 传播 的特定 暴发 和 监测 和响应能力的巨大差距。加强实验室监测、病例检测和公平的疫苗准入可以支持暴发控制。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 猴痘病毒 (正痘病毒 猴痘)、MPXV 分支和 猴痘 疾病。核心问题是综合非洲国家的 流行病学、传播 动态和 猴痘 暴发 的系统性挑战。
证据基础与方法
审查遵循 PRISMA 2020 指南。关于 猴痘 流行病学、病死率和公共卫生应对措施的研究和报告来自 PubMed 以及世界卫生组织和非洲疾病预防控制中心等机构来源。发表资格窗口为2020年1月1日至2024年10月31日。其中包括2022年至2024年期间发表的六个来源。调查结果以叙述方式综合。
共识与结论
世卫组织报告,2024年1月1日至10月6日,非洲各地确诊7,535例猴痘病例,其中刚果民主共和国(DRC)有6,169例。来自刚果民主共和国东部的证据与 Ib 分支的持续人传人 传播 一致。审查后的报告发现,病例检测、诊断能力和 疫苗 访问方面存在巨大差距。
争议与缺口
审查发现了病例检测、诊断能力和 疫苗 访问方面的差距。证据中没有明确提及相互矛盾的发现或争议。
科研与实践启示
加强实验室监测、病例检测和公平的疫苗准入可以支持暴发控制。该审查强调需要改进 监测 和响应能力。

Systematic Review of Mpox Outbreaks in Africa: Epidemiology, Transmission Dynamics, and Challenges

Authors: Suandy Suandy, Anjas Domini Sidabutar
Original abstract (English)
Pendahuluan: Mpox, yang sebelumnya dikenal sebagai cacar monyet (monkeypox), telah muncul sebagai ancaman kesehatan masyarakat yang kian berkembang di seluruh Afrika. Ancaman ini ditandai dengan meningkatnya penularan antarmanusia, perluasan cakupan geografis, serta berbagai tantangan kesehatan masyarakat yang signifikan. Tinjauan sistematis ini bertujuan untuk menyintesis epidemiologi, dinamika penularan, dan tantangan sistemik terkait wabah Mpox di negara-negara Afrika. Metode: Pelaporan disusun berdasarkan pedoman PRISMA 2020. Pencarian studi dan laporan mengenai epidemiologi Mpox, tingkat kematian kasus (case fatality rate), serta respons kesehatan masyarakat dilakukan melalui PubMed dan sumber-sumber kelembagaan, termasuk WHO dan Africa CDC. Rentang waktu publikasi yang disertakan adalah 1 Januari 2020 hingga 31 Oktober 2024. Sebanyak enam sumber yang diterbitkan antara tahun 2022 dan 2024 dimasukkan dalam tinjauan ini. Temuan disintesis secara naratif. Hasil: WHO melaporkan 7.535 kasus terkonfirmasi di seluruh Afrika dari tanggal 1 Januari hingga 6 Oktober 2024, dengan 6.169 kasus di antaranya terjadi di Republik Demokratik Kongo (RDK). Bukti dari wilayah timur RDK menunjukkan adanya penularan antarmanusia yang berkelanjutan dari klad Ib. Laporan yang ditinjau mengidentifikasi adanya kesenjangan dalam deteksi kasus, kapasitas diagnostik, dan akses terhadap vaksin. Kesimpulan: Bukti yang ditinjau mendokumentasikan terjadinya penularan antarmanusia yang berkelanjutan pada wabah-wabah tertentu, serta kesenjangan yang substansial dalam kapasitas surveilans dan respons. Penguatan surveilans laboratorium, deteksi kasus, dan akses vaksin yang adil dapat mendukung upaya pengendalian wabah. Introduction: Mpox, formerly known as monkeypox, has emerged as a growing public health threat across Africa, characterized by increasing human-to-human transmission, geographic expansion, and significant public health challenges. This systematic review aimed to synthesize the epidemiology, transmission dynamics, and systemic challenges of Mpox outbreaks across African countries. Method: Reporting was guided by PRISMA 2020. PubMed and institutional sources, including WHO and Africa CDC, were searched for studies and reports on Mpox epidemiology, case fatality rates, and public health responses. The publication eligibility window was 1 January 2020 to 31 October 2024. Six sources published between 2022 and 2024 were included. Findings were synthesized narratively. Results: WHO reported 7,535 confirmed cases across Africa from 1 January to 6 October 2024, including 6,169 in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). Evidence from eastern DRC was consistent with sustained human-to-human transmission of clade Ib. The reviewed reports identified gaps in case detection, diagnostic capacity, and vaccine access. Conclusion: The reviewed evidence documents sustained human transmission in specific outbreaks and substantial gaps in surveillance and response capacity. Strengthening laboratory surveillance, case detection, and equitable vaccine access can support outbreak control.

Scope and question
The review focuses on Mpox virus (Orthopoxvirus monkeypox), MPXV clades, and mpox disease. The central question is to synthesize the epidemiology, transmission dynamics, and systemic challenges of Mpox outbreaks across African countries.
Evidence base and method
The review followed PRISMA 2020 guidelines. Studies and reports on Mpox epidemiology, case fatality rates, and public health responses were sourced from PubMed and institutional sources such as WHO and Africa CDC. The publication eligibility window was from 1 January 2020 to 31 October 2024. Six sources published between 2022 and 2024 were included. Findings were synthesized narratively.
Consensus and conclusions
WHO reported 7,535 confirmed Mpox cases across Africa from 1 January to 6 October 2024, with 6,169 cases in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). Evidence from eastern DRC was consistent with sustained human-to-human transmission of clade Ib. The reviewed reports identified substantial gaps in case detection, diagnostic capacity, and vaccine access.
Controversies and gaps
The review identified gaps in case detection, diagnostic capacity, and vaccine access. There is no explicit mention of conflicting findings or controversies in the evidence.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening laboratory surveillance, case detection, and equitable vaccine access can support outbreak control. The review highlights the need for improved surveillance and response capacity.

撒哈拉以南非洲地区医护人员应对猴痘大流行的准备情况:快速回顾调查知识差距

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap
作者： Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
摘要中文翻译背景:猴痘 是一种人畜共患病毒性疾病,由撒哈拉以南非洲 (SSA) 流行的 正痘病毒 引起,该病的死灰复燃凸显了改善卫生保健工作者 (HCW) 准备情况的迫切需要。尽管“同一个健康”方法具有潜力,但目前的实施仍然分散。本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。方法:按照系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。纳入了评估 HCW 知识、态度或 SSA 中 猴痘 准备情况的研究,并提供英文或法文全文;那些涉及非医护人员人群、在 SSA 之外进行或缺乏主要数据的研究被排除在外。两名评审员使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。结果:纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。结论:尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。

证据基础与方法
遵循系统评价和荟萃分析的首选报告项目 (PRISMA) 指南,使用 MeSH 术语和布尔运算符对 2000 年至 2024 年间发表的研究进行 PubMed 和 ScienceDirect 搜索。两位评价者使用标准化模板独立筛选和提取数据;使用 JBI 分析横断面研究批判性评估清单评估质量。
共识与结论
纳入了来自 388 份记录的五项研究(喀麦隆、埃塞俄比亚、尼日利亚、刚果民主共和国;n = 1,672 名医护人员)。只有 46.8% 的医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识。障碍包括对社交媒体的依赖和获得正规培训的机会有限。对预防的积极态度与较高的知识水平相关。
争议与缺口
尽管该地区的病死率接近全球平均水平的十倍,但只有不到一半的 SSA 医护人员表现出足够的 猴痘 知识;积极态度并未转化为临床准备,反映了严重的意向与行动差距;社交媒体作为信息来源占据主导地位,强调了在同一个健康培训框架内需要经过同行评审的替代方案。

本快速审查综合了 HCW 的知识、态度、信息来源以及 SSA 中 猴痘 准备障碍的证据,以确定培训需求和政策影响。

Healthcare Workers’ Preparedness for Monkeypox Pandemic Response in SubSaharan Africa: A Rapid Review Investigating the Knowledge Gap

Authors: Arole Darwin Touko, Ruth Tabi Amin, Gordon Takop Nchanji
Original abstract (English)
Background: Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs). Despite the potential of a One Health approach, current implementations remain fragmented. This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications. Methods: Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Studies assessing HCW knowledge, attitudes, or preparedness for Mpox in SSA with full-text availability in English or French were included; those involving non-HCW populations, conducted outside SSA, or lacking primary data were excluded. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical CrossSectional Studies. Results: Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels. Conclusion: Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox is a zoonotic viral disease caused by an Orthopoxvirus endemic to sub-Saharan Africa (SSA), where its resurgence underscores the urgent need for improved preparedness among health care workers (HCWs).
Evidence base and method
Following Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses (PRISMA) guidelines, PubMed and ScienceDirect were searched using MeSH terms and Boolean operators for studies published between 2000 and 2024. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data using a standardised template; quality was assessed using the JBI Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical CrossSectional Studies.
Consensus and conclusions
Five studies from 388 records were included (Cameroon, Ethiopia, Nigeria, DRC; n = 1,672 HCWs). Only 46.8% of HCWs demonstrated adequate Mpox knowledge. Barriers included reliance on social media and limited access to formal training. Positive attitudes towards prevention correlated with higher knowledge levels.
Controversies and gaps
Fewer than half of SSA HCWs demonstrate adequate Mpox knowledge despite a regional case fatality rate nearly ten times the global average; positive attitudes are not translating into clinical preparedness, reflecting a critical intention-action gap; and social media dominates as an information source, underscoring the need for peer-reviewed alternatives within a One Health training framework.
Research and practice implications
This rapid review synthesises evidence on HCWs' knowledge, attitudes, information sources, and barriers to preparedness regarding Mpox in SSA to identify training needs and policy implications.

基于 CRISPR-Cas 的 猴痘病毒 核酸检测的准确性系统评价和荟萃分析

Accuracy of CRISPR-Cas-based nucleic acid detection for Mpox Virus a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Zhenyang Huang
摘要中文翻译猴痘 是一种由猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 引起的新出现和重新出现的传染病,对全球公共卫生构成持续威胁。早期、快速、准确地检测 MPXV 对于及时 诊断、适当的临床管理和有效的 暴发 预防和控制至关重要。实时定量聚合酶链式反应 (qPCR) 被广泛用作 MPXV 核酸检测的参考方法。然而,它对专业设备、训练有素的人员和实验室基础设施的依赖限制了其在资源有限的环境和即时检测(POCT)应用中的可及性。近年来,由于其高特异性、可编程性、快速检测能力以及与等温核酸扩增技术集成的潜力,簇状规则间隔短回文重复序列(CRISPR)相关(Cas)系统已成为有前途的分子诊断平台。各种基于 CRISPR-Cas 的检测方法(包括 Cas12 和 Cas13 介导的检测系统)已被开发用于 MPXV 检测。尽管个别研究报告了令人鼓舞的诊断性能,但他们的研究结果在敏感性、特异性、扩增策略、检测形式和临床验证方法方面存在差异。因此,需要进行全面的系统评价和荟萃分析,以定量评估基于CRISPR-Cas的MPXV核酸检测方法的诊断准确性,并调查异质性的潜在来源。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
猴痘 是一种由猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 引起的新出现和重新出现的传染病,对全球公共卫生构成持续威胁。该综述旨在评估基于 CRISPR-Cas 的 MPXV 核酸检测方法的诊断准确性,并调查异质性的潜在来源。
证据基础与方法
需要进行全面的系统评价和荟萃分析,以定量评估基于 CRISPR-Cas 的 MPXV 核酸检测方法的诊断准确性,并调查异质性的潜在来源。
共识与结论
尽管个别研究报告了令人鼓舞的诊断性能,但他们的研究结果在敏感性、特异性、扩增策略、检测形式和临床验证方法方面存在差异。
争议与缺口
实时定量聚合酶链式反应 (qPCR) 被广泛用作 MPXV 核酸检测的参考方法。然而,它对专业设备、训练有素的人员和实验室基础设施的依赖限制了其在资源有限的环境和即时检测 (POCT) 应用中的可访问性。近年来,成簇规则间隔短回文重复序列(CRISPR)相关(Cas)系统因其高特异性、可编程性、快速检测能力以及与等温核酸扩增技术集成的潜力而成为有前途的分子诊断平台。
科研与实践启示
早期、快速、准确地检测 MPXV 对于及时 诊断、适当的临床管理和有效的 暴发 预防和控制至关重要。

Accuracy of CRISPR-Cas-based nucleic acid detection for Mpox Virus a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Zhenyang Huang
Original abstract (English)
Mpox is an emerging and re-emerging infectious disease caused by the monkeypox virus (MPXV), posing an ongoing threat to global public health. Early, rapid, and accurate detection of MPXV is essential for timely diagnosis, appropriate clinical management, and effective outbreak prevention and control. Quantitative real-time polymerase chain reaction (qPCR) is widely used as the reference method for MPXV nucleic acid detection. However, its dependence on specialized equipment, trained personnel, and laboratory infrastructure limits its accessibility in resource-limited settings and point-of-care testing (POCT) applications.​ In recent years, clustered regularly interspaced short palindromic repeats (CRISPR)-associated (Cas) systems have emerged as promising molecular diagnostic platforms owing to their high specificity, programmability, rapid detection capabilities, and potential for integration with isothermal nucleic acid amplification technologies. Various CRISPR-Cas-based assays, including Cas12- and Cas13-mediated detection systems, have been developed for MPXV detection. Although individual studies have reported encouraging diagnostic performance, their findings vary in terms of sensitivity, specificity, amplification strategies, detection formats, and clinical validation methods. Therefore, a comprehensive systematic review and meta-analysis is needed to quantitatively evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of CRISPR-Cas-based nucleic acid detection methods for MPXV and to investigate potential sources of heterogeneity.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Mpox is an emerging and re-emerging infectious disease caused by the monkeypox virus (MPXV), posing an ongoing threat to global public health. The review aims to evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of CRISPR-Cas-based nucleic acid detection methods for MPXV and investigate potential sources of heterogeneity.
Evidence base and method
A comprehensive systematic review and meta-analysis is needed to quantitatively evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of CRISPR-Cas-based nucleic acid detection methods for MPXV and to investigate potential sources of heterogeneity.
Consensus and conclusions
Although individual studies have reported encouraging diagnostic performance, their findings vary in terms of sensitivity, specificity, amplification strategies, detection formats, and clinical validation methods.
Controversies and gaps
Quantitative real-time polymerase chain reaction (qPCR) is widely used as the reference method for MPXV nucleic acid detection. However, its dependence on specialized equipment, trained personnel, and laboratory infrastructure limits its accessibility in resource-limited settings and point-of-care testing (POCT) applications. In recent years, clustered regularly interspaced short palindromic repeats (CRISPR)-associated (Cas) systems have emerged as promising molecular diagnostic platforms owing to their high specificity, programmability, rapid detection capabilities, and potential for integration with isothermal nucleic acid amplification technologies.
Research and practice implications
Early, rapid, and accurate detection of MPXV is essential for timely diagnosis, appropriate clinical management, and effective outbreak prevention and control.

在 疫苗 研究中应用目标试验仿真:范围界定审查

Applying Target Trial Emulation in Vaccine Research: A Scoping Review
作者： Hao Dai, Tien-Yu Chang, Xing He, Amanda Eiden, Wei (Vivian) Wang, Kelsie Cassell, Yi-Ling Huang, Yu-Han Kao, Dong Wang, Fangyu Li
摘要中文翻译重要性:目标试验模拟(TTE)已成为加强观察性研究中因果推断的严谨框架,其遵循随机临床试验的设计原则,使用临床数据。尽管TTE在疫苗疗效和安全性评估中应用日益增多,但其实施、报告实践以及与真实世界证据监管标准的一致性仍缺乏充分描述。目的:评估模拟目标试验的观察性疫苗研究在方法学严谨性、报告完整性以及与监管真实世界证据指南的一致性。证据审查:在PubMed、Embase和Web of Science中搜索了2021年1月至2025年8月期间发表的同行评审研究,这些研究使用临床数据评估疫苗的疗效或安全性。符合纳入标准的研究通过比较性或基于因果推断的设计,显式或隐式地模拟了目标试验。两名评审员独立筛选并提取了关于遵循TTE核心设计要素(即:纳入标准、治疗策略、分配程序、随访、结局、因果对比和分析计划)以及与监管真实世界证据指南一致性的数据。发现:在79项符合纳入标准的研究中,58项涉及新冠(COVID-19),19项涉及流感,12项涉及其他疫苗可预防疾病,包括带状疱疹、猴痘、肺炎球菌病、人乳头瘤病毒以及其它疾病。仅有25项研究(31.6%)明确报告了可追溯的协议。在偏倚评估和缓解方面,61项研究(77.2%)明确界定了时间零,这是减少不朽时间偏倚和选择偏倚的关键步骤,而26项研究(32.9%)使用了负对照来评估残留偏倚,仅有2项研究(2.5%)使用E值进行定量偏倚分析。尽管监管机构强调数据可信度,但仅有5项研究(6.3%)进行了系统性的数据质量评估。结论和相关性:本研究中,方法学严谨性和透明度差异较大,明确的协议、经过验证的操作定义以及系统性的数据质量评估均较为罕见。为了提高可重复性和监管可信度,未来的研究应优先采用标准化的TTE报告框架,确保明确的协议,并纳入数据质量评估。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
评估模拟目标试验的观察性 疫苗 研究的方法严谨性、报告完整性和监管一致性,重点关注 猴痘病毒(猴痘病毒,正痘病毒 猴痘)。
证据基础与方法
在PubMed、Embase和Web of Science中搜索了2021年1月至2025年8月期间发表的同行评审研究,这些研究使用临床数据评估疫苗的疗效或安全性,共有79项研究符合纳入标准,其中58项涉及新冠(COVID-19),19项涉及流感,12项涉及其他疫苗可预防疾病,包括猴痘。
共识与结论
在 79 项研究中,只有 25 项(31.6%)报告了明确记录的方案,61 项(77.2%)明确定义了零时间以减少偏差,而系统数据质量评估仅出现在 5 项研究(6.3%)中。
争议与缺口
方法的严谨性和透明度差异很大,记录的协议、经过验证的操作定义和系统的数据质量评估都不常见。研究中没有系统地解决监管对数据可信度的重视。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应优先采用标准化的 TTE 报告框架,确保明确记录协议,并纳入数据质量评估,以提高可重复性和监管可信度。

Applying Target Trial Emulation in Vaccine Research: A Scoping Review

Authors: Hao Dai, Tien-Yu Chang, Xing He, Amanda Eiden, Wei (Vivian) Wang, Kelsie Cassell, Yi-Ling Huang, Yu-Han Kao, Dong Wang, Fangyu Li
Original abstract (English)
Importance: Target trial emulation (TTE) has emerged as a rigorous framework for strengthening causal inference in observational research, following the design principles of randomized clinical trials using clinical data. While TTE is increasingly applied in vaccine effectiveness and safety evaluations, its implementation, reporting practices, and alignment with regulatory standards for real-world evidence remain poorly characterized. Objective: To evaluate the methodological rigor, reporting completeness, and regulatory alignment of observational vaccine studies that emulate a target trial. Evidence Review: PubMed, Embase, and Web of Science were searched for peer-reviewed studies published between January 2021 and August 2025 that used clinical data to assess vaccine effectiveness or safety. Eligible studies explicitly or implicitly emulated a target trial through comparative, causal inference-based designs. Two reviewers independently screened and extracted data on adherence to TTE core design components (ie, eligibility, treatment strategies, assignment procedures, follow-up, outcomes, causal contrasts, and analysis plans) and alignment with regulatory real-world evidence guidance. Findings: Among 79 studies meeting inclusion criteria, 58 covered COVID-19, 19 covered influenza, and 12 covered other vaccine-preventable diseases, including herpes zoster, mpox, pneumococcal disease, human papillomavirus, and other diseases. Only 25 studies (31.6%) reported clearly documented protocols. For bias assessment and mitigation, 61 studies (77.2%) explicitly defined time zero, a key step in reducing immortal time and selection biases, while 26 studies (32.9%) used negative control to assess residual bias and only 2 studies (2.5%) used quantitative bias analysis using E-values. Despite regulatory emphasis on data credibility, systematic data quality assessments appeared in only 5 studies (6.3%). Conclusions and Relevance: In this study, methodological rigor and transparency varied widely, with documented protocols, validated operational definitions, and systematic data quality assessments all uncommon. To improve reproducibility and regulatory credibility, future research should prioritize adopting standardized TTE reporting frameworks, ensure clearly documented protocols, and incorporate data quality assessments.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
To evaluate the methodological rigor, reporting completeness, and regulatory alignment of observational vaccine studies that emulate a target trial, focusing on mpox virus (monkeypox virus, Orthopoxvirus monkeypox).
Evidence base and method
PubMed, Embase, and Web of Science were searched for peer-reviewed studies published between January 2021 and August 2025 that used clinical data to assess vaccine effectiveness or safety, with 79 studies meeting inclusion criteria, 58 covering COVID-19, 19 covering influenza, and 12 covering other vaccine-preventable diseases including mpox.
Consensus and conclusions
Among 79 studies, only 25 (31.6%) reported clearly documented protocols, and 61 (77.2%) explicitly defined time zero to reduce biases, while systematic data quality assessments appeared in only 5 studies (6.3%).
Controversies and gaps
Methodological rigor and transparency varied widely, with documented protocols, validated operational definitions, and systematic data quality assessments all uncommon. Regulatory emphasis on data credibility was not systematically addressed in the studies.
Research and practice implications
Future research should prioritize adopting standardized TTE reporting frameworks, ensuring clearly documented protocols, and incorporating data quality assessments to improve reproducibility and regulatory credibility.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

用于传染病的 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 疫苗:作用机制和转化进展

mRNA-Lipid Nanoparticle Vaccines for Infectious Diseases: Mechanisms of Action and Translational Progress
作者： Yunwang Zhao, 程宏献, Shiyi Zhou, Lei He, Chenlin Xiu, Baihai Huang, Xiongxiong Lu, Cheng H
摘要中文翻译在首次获得紧急使用授权六年后,mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒(mRNA-LNP)疫苗已经从一个没有许可产品的平台转变为供应全球大部分COVID-19和呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)疫苗剂量的平台,并已完成针对季节性流感的3期试验。它们的性能取决于在个体细胞类型、受体和分子相互作用水平上以异常速度解决的机制,并且在此过程中必须修改一些教科书的假设。本次审查遵循 疫苗 从配方瓶到生发中心的整个过程。我们首先描述了如何设计 RNA 以实现有效翻译并部分逃离胞质和内体 RNA 传感器,以及为什么 N1-甲基假尿苷既不是免疫沉默也不是翻译中性。然后,我们研究可电离脂质、辅助磷脂、胆固醇和 PEG-脂质如何决定颗粒行为、细胞摄取和内体释放,以及脂质和 RNA 成分如何充当可分离的佐剂信号。比较了来自小鼠模型和人类淋巴结采样的证据,并注意先天细胞因子控制方面的巨大物种差异,这限制了从小鼠到人类的外推。临床部分总结了 COVID-19、RSV 和流感 疫苗 的疗效和免疫原性数据,以及针对巨细胞病毒、猴痘、HIV、疟疾、尼帕病毒和艰难梭菌的候选药物的临床前和早期临床状态。安全数据以绝对术语而不是单独作为信号来讨论。我们以目前阻碍合理改进的问题作为结束语:脂质传感器的分子特性、将免疫原性与反应原性分开的困难、缺乏内体逃逸的定量描述以及不耐受单一固定免疫原的病原体的抗原设计。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注用于感染性疾病的 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 (mRNA-LNP) 疫苗,特别重点关注 猴痘病毒。核心问题是这些 疫苗 如何发挥作用,以及它们在对抗 猴痘 和相关疾病方面的转化进展。
证据基础与方法
该综述是 2026 年发表的预印本,全面概述了 mRNA-LNP 疫苗 的开发。它包括来自小鼠模型和人类淋巴结采样的证据,并关注先天细胞因子控制的物种差异。该综述没有明确报告荟萃分析或汇总估计,而是综合了各种研究的结果。
共识与结论
mRNA-LNP 疫苗 已经从一个没有许可产品的平台转变为广泛用于治疗 猴痘 等疾病的平台。它强调了了解 RNA 翻译的分子机制和脂质纳米颗粒行为对于 疫苗 开发的重要性。
争议与缺口
关于脂质传感器的分子特性以及区分免疫原性和反应原性的难度存在争议。此外,该评论指出,缺乏对内体逃逸的定量描述,以及为不耐受单一固定免疫原的病原体设计抗原时面临的挑战。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,未来的研究应侧重于识别脂质传感器的分子身份,改善免疫原性与反应原性的分离,量化内体逃逸,以及开发不耐受单一固定免疫原的病原体的抗原。

mRNA-Lipid Nanoparticle Vaccines for Infectious Diseases: Mechanisms of Action and Translational Progress

Authors: Yunwang Zhao, 程宏献, Shiyi Zhou, Lei He, Chenlin Xiu, Baihai Huang, Xiongxiong Lu, Cheng H
Original abstract (English)
Six years after the first emergency use authorizations, mRNA-lipid nanoparticle (mRNA-LNP) vaccines have gone from a platform without a licensed product to one that supplies a large share of the world's COVID-19 and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccine doses and has completed phase 3 trials for seasonal influenza. Their performance depends on mechanisms that are being resolved with unusual speed at the level of individual cell types, receptors and molecular interactions, and several textbook assumptions have had to be revised in the process. This review follows the vaccine from the formulation vial to the germinal centre. We first describe how the RNA is engineered for efficient translation and for partial escape from cytosolic and endosomal RNA sensors, and why N1-methylpseudouridine is neither immunologically silent nor translationally neutral. We then examine how the ionizable lipid, the helper phospholipid, cholesterol and the PEG-lipid determine particle behaviour, cell uptake and endosomal release, and how the lipid and RNA components act as separable adjuvant signals. Evidence from mouse models and from human lymph node sampling is compared, with attention to the substantial species differences in innate cytokine control that limit extrapolation from mice to people. The clinical section summarizes efficacy and immunogenicity data for COVID-19, RSV and influenza vaccines, and the preclinical and early clinical status of candidates against cytomegalovirus, mpox, HIV, malaria, Nipah virus and Clostridioides difficile. Safety data are discussed in absolute terms rather than as signals alone. We close with the questions that currently block rational improvement: the molecular identity of the lipid sensor, the difficulty of separating immunogenicity from reactogenicity, the absence of a quantitative description of endosomal escape, and the design of antigens for pathogens that do not tolerate a single fixed immunogen.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on mRNA-Lipid Nanoparticle (mRNA-LNP) vaccines for infectious diseases, with a specific emphasis on the Mpox virus. The central question is how these vaccines function mechanistically and their translational progress in combating Mpox and related diseases.
Evidence base and method
The review is a preprint published in 2026, which provides a comprehensive overview of mRNA-LNP vaccine development. It includes evidence from mouse models and human lymph node sampling, with attention to species differences in innate cytokine control. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates but synthesizes findings from various studies.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that mRNA-LNP vaccines have transitioned from a platform without a licensed product to one that is widely used for diseases like Mpox. It highlights the importance of understanding the molecular mechanisms of RNA translation and lipid nanoparticle behavior for vaccine development.
Controversies and gaps
There are controversies regarding the molecular identity of the lipid sensor and the difficulty of separating immunogenicity from reactogenicity. Additionally, the review notes the absence of a quantitative description of endosomal escape and challenges in designing antigens for pathogens that do not tolerate a single fixed immunogen.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on identifying the molecular identity of the lipid sensor, improving the separation of immunogenicity from reactogenicity, quantifying endosomal escape, and developing antigens for pathogens that do not tolerate a single fixed immunogen.

Mpox cases persist in U.S., but public awareness remains low - Annenberg Public Policy Center

时间

猴痘 病例在美国持续存在,但公众对该病毒的认识和了解仍然很低。这是安纳伯格公共政策中心报道的。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox cases persist in U.S., but public awareness remains low - Annenberg Public Policy Center

News Brief
The Annenberg Public Policy Center reports that mpox cases continue to persist in the U.S., but public awareness and knowledge about the virus remain low. The analysis, published on November 19, 2026, highlights that the situation has not changed significantly over the past two years. While the report does not provide specific case or death counts, it emphasizes the ongoing presence of mpox and the lack of public understanding, which could pose risks for underreporting or insufficient prevention measures. The study focuses on communication and public health awareness rather than detailing direct interventions or containment strategies. The findings are based on general observations and surveys, without explicit evidence of targeted methods or results.

调查显示,美国 猴痘 案件持续存在,公众意识仍然较低 - Bioengineer.org

Survey Shows Public Awareness Remains Low as U.S. Mpox Cases Persist - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A survey conducted by the Annenberg Public Policy Center in late 2026 reveals that public awareness of mpox in the United States remains low, with only 8 percent of adults reporting awareness of the virus in the preceding month. This is a slight decline from 11 percent in July 2024 but a dramatic drop from 80 percent in July 2022, when the global outbreak was at its peak. The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) noted that mpox continues to circulate at a low level, with 1,369 cases confirmed in 2026 as of October 24. Despite some improvements in understanding specific transmission routes, such as placental transmission, the overall public knowledge about mpox has stagnated. The CDC emphasized that the virus spreads through close contact and contaminated materials, and that it has transitioned toward endemic circulation in the U.S. The survey highlights the need for improved public education and awareness about mpox, as knowledge gaps persist despite the virus's ongoing presence.

Survey Shows Public Awareness Remains Low as U.S. Mpox Cases Persist - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A survey conducted by the Annenberg Public Policy Center in late 2026 reveals that public awareness of mpox in the United States remains low, with only 8 percent of adults reporting awareness of the virus in the preceding month. This is a slight decline from 11 percent in July 2024 but a dramatic drop from 80 percent in July 2022, when the global outbreak was at its peak. The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) noted that mpox continues to circulate at a low level, with 1,369 cases confirmed in 2026 as of October 24. Despite some improvements in understanding specific transmission routes, such as placental transmission, the overall public knowledge about mpox has stagnated. The CDC emphasized that the virus spreads through close contact and contaminated materials, and that it has transitioned toward endemic circulation in the U.S. The survey highlights the need for improved public education and awareness about mpox, as knowledge gaps persist despite the virus's ongoing presence.

HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Monday, November 23, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Survey Shows Public Awareness Remains Low as U.S. Mpox Cases Persist by November 19, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 5 mins read 0 Survey Shows Public Awareness Remains Low as U.S. Mpox Cases Persist Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Four years following the major global outbreak in 2022, the United States continues to record a persistent, low level of mpox cases, yet American public awareness and knowledge concerning the viral disease have changed little over the past two years according to a new nationally representative survey released by the Annenberg Public Policy Center of the University of Pennsylvania. In the latest survey wave, concluded in October 2026, only 8 percent of adults in the United States reported having seen, read, or heard anything about mpox during the preceding month. This figure represents a slight decline from the 11 percent observed in July 2024 and marks a dramatic reduction from the 80 percent recorded in July 2022, when the initial global outbreak drew intensive national and international media coverage. According to the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention, domestic transmission patterns have evolved since the acute surge in 2022, with nationwide case counts falling substantially while a steady baseline of infections has endured. The agency has noted that this continued low-level transmission indicates a probable transition toward endemic viral circulation within the country. Federal surveillance data cited in the report show that 1,369 cases of mpox, historically referred to as monkeypox, had been officially confirmed and reported across the United States in 2026 as of October 24. The public health agency emphasizes that the mpox virus spreads person-to-person primarily through close contact, including intimate or sexual contact, with the rash, scabs, or bodily fluids of an infected individual. Secondary transmission routes highlighted by the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention include physical contact with contaminated materials such as shared clothing, bedding, towels, and dishes. Furthermore, exposure can occur through direct interaction with small wild animals indigenous to West and Central Africa, where the pathogen has long been established. Discover more Check Symptoms Read Med Journals Study Biology public health The survey findings stem from the Annenberg Science and Public Health survey conducted between September 22 and October 5, 2026, among a sample of 1,842 empaneled American adults. Researchers found that widespread knowledge deficits documented during their 2024 assessment remain largely entrenched, following a steep drop from the heightened public attentiveness of 2022. Overall familiarity with the viral infection is notably limited, with merely 24 percent of respondents indicating that they are somewhat or very familiar with mpox, which is statistically unchanged from the 23 percent reported in July 2024. Conversely, 76 percent of surveyed adults indicated that they are not too familiar or not at all familiar with the illness, while only 7 percent expressed worry about personally contracting mpox over the subsequent three months, compared with 5 percent in 2024 and 21 percent in August 2022. General comprehension regarding preventive medical interventions has similarly stagnated over the two-year monitoring interval. Less than half of respondents, or 44 percent, were aware that an effective vaccine against mpox is currently available, a figure virtually identical to the 45 percent measured in 2024 and substantially lower than the 61 percent recorded in August 2022. Nearly half of the public, representing 48 percent, stated that they were unsure whether any vaccine existed. Additionally, only 18 percent of participants correctly identified mpox as being less contagious than the virus causing Covid-19, essentially matching the 17 percent measured in 2024, while 62 percent admitted uncertainty, contrasting with the 41 percent who correctly understood relative contagiousness during the 2022 outbreak. Public recognition of groups facing disproportionate epidemiological risk also showed no meaningful gain. Approximately one-third of respondents, or 33 percent, correctly recognized that men who have sex with men face a higher risk of acquiring an mpox infection, mirroring the 34 percent documented in 2024 and standing at roughly half of the 63 percent who recognized this vulnerability in August 2022, with 53 percent remaining unsure. Awareness of specific transmission pathways showed comparable stagnation: 49 percent recognized that sharing bedding, towels, or clothing used by an infected patient elevates infection risks, compared with 48 percent in 2024, while 51 percent recognized the infection hazard posed by close face-to-face interaction, compared with 49 percent in the previous survey. The persistence of these public knowledge deficits presents distinct public health challenges as the viral pathogen continues to circulate. Ken Winneg, managing director of survey research at the Annenberg Public Policy Center, noted that the stability of these gaps is concerning because the pathogen itself has not stood still. Winneg underscored that as the epidemiology of mpox continues to shift over time, broader public understanding regarding vulnerable populations, viral transmission pathways, and the preventive protection offered by immunization must keep pace with ongoing disease dynamics. Discover more Track Outbreaks Public Health Find Treatments infections infectious diseases Despite broad stagnation across many survey metrics, the research team identified measurable gains in two specific public health domains. Knowledge that the mpox virus can be transmitted through the placenta of an infected pregnant individual rose to 29 percent among American adults, up from 21 percent in July 2024, although 63 percent of respondents still reported being unsure. The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention confirms that the virus can transfer to a developing fetus during pregnancy, potentially generating significant health complications for both the pregnant parent and the infant. Concurrently, public awareness regarding recommended infection control practices improved, with 73 percent recognizing that infected individuals should isolate at home until all skin rashes have resolved, compared with 68 percent in 2024. The federal health agency specifically advises patients to remain in isolation until rashes have fully healed, scabs have completely sloughed off, and an intact layer of fresh skin has developed. Intentions to accept vaccination in the event of confirmed exposure remain robust among the American public. The survey revealed that 69 percent of adults would be somewhat or very likely to receive an mpox vaccine if exposed to the pathogen, matching the 68 percent reported in 2024, while an additional 2 percent reported having already received vaccination. Notably, the share of respondents indicating that they would be very likely to seek vaccination climbed significantly from 38 percent in 2024 to 44 percent in the current survey. Furthermore, 73 percent of respondents affirmed that the clinical benefits of mpox vaccination outweigh potential risks, compared with 70 percent in 2024, a change that did not reach statistical significance. In its clinical guidance, the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention recommends a two-dose regimen of the JYNNEOS vaccine to achieve maximum protection among recommended populations, noting that the vaccine provides substantial disease prevention and can be administered post-exposure to avert illness. The survey also assessed public susceptibility to unverified narratives and medical falsehoods surrounding mpox origin and immunization. Belief in the unsubstantiated claim that the mpox virus was bioengineered in a laboratory setting was endorsed by 16 percent of respondents as definitely or probably true, showing no meaningful change from 15 percent in 2024, with 41 percent unsure and 43 percent correctly evaluating the statement as false. FactCheck.org, an initiative of the Annenberg Public Policy Center, has extensively debunked this claim. Meanwhile, 7 percent of respondents mistakenly believed that receiving a Covid-19 vaccine increases susceptibility to mpox, statistically unchanged from 6 percent in 2024. However, 63 percent correctly rejected this false claim, improving from 58 percent in 2024, while 30 percent expressed uncertainty. Wave 31 of the Annenberg Science and Public Health survey was administered from September 22 to October 5, 2026, through SSRS, an independent research organization. The study gathered data from 1,842 adults nationwide using both web-based and telephone interviews drawn from the probability-based SSRS Opinion Panel, with post-stratification weighting applied to align results with United States adult demographic parameters. The survey carries a margin of sampling error of plus or minus 3.2 percentage points. The Annenberg Public Policy Center, established in 1993, has monitored public knowledge, beliefs, and behaviors regarding infectious diseases and health communication since April 2021, with this survey project conducted by Winneg alongside Patrick E. Jamieson, Laura A. Gibson, and Kathleen Hall Jamieson. 美国全国 监测 跟踪 猴痘 Clade IIb 进化 和 Clade Ib 介绍 - Bioengineer.org

Nationwide US Surveillance Tracks Mpox Clade IIb Evolution and Clade Ib Introductions - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报美国的一项全国性 基因组监测 计划追踪了 猴痘病毒 进化枝 IIb 的 进化,并确定了进化枝 Ib 的两次单独引入。该研究于 2026 年 11 月 19 日发表,分析了 2024 年 8 月至 2025 年 6 月期间收集的临床标本中的 326 个高质量全 基因组 序列。Labcorp 在美国卫生与公众服务部的所有 10 个区域进行的 监测 揭示了进化枝 IIb 的 12 个不同的循环谱系和显着的结构改变,包括反向末端重复附近的大缺失。突变分析显示了 APOBEC3 胞苷脱氨酶介导的 宿主 编辑的明显特征,为人与人之间持续存在 传播 提供了分子证据。该方法使用 Pacific Biosciences Sequel II 技术和分子环探针,并通过疾病控制和预防中心的并行宏基因组测序和定制计算模拟进行了验证。研究结果强调了 基因组监测 在理解 猴痘病毒 进化 以及为未来 暴发 做好准备方面的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
监测于2024年8月至2025年6月进行,并于2025年1月至2025年6月检测到分支Ib引入。发布日期为2026年11月19日。
地点与对象
监测 在美国卫生与公众服务部所有十个地区进行,涉及 Labcorp 收集的临床样本。受影响人群包括 猴痘病毒 感染者。
事件
一项全国性的 基因组监测 计划追踪了 猴痘病毒 进化枝 IIb 的 进化,并确定了进化枝 Ib 的两个单独引入。该研究由 Bioengineer.org 于 2026 年 11 月 19 日报道,并根据基因组分析得到证实。
规模、影响与风险
监测 捕获了 clade IIb 的 12 个不同的循环谱系和两个 clade Ib 引入。它还发现了病毒的显着结构改变和突变模式,表明正在进行的 进化 和人与人之间的 传播。该研究没有报告具体病例或死亡人数,但强调了监测基因突变对 暴发 风险的重要性。
应对、状态与不确定性
监测 平台是利用现有的 SARS-CoV-2 测序基础设施建立的,并通过疾病控制和预防中心的并行宏基因组测序进行验证。长读长测序方法被证实能够可靠区分已知的 猴痘 进化枝和谱系。该研究正在进行中,为未来的传染病监测提供了经过验证的技术基础。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Nationwide US Surveillance Tracks Mpox Clade IIb Evolution and Clade Ib Introductions - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A nationwide genomic surveillance initiative in the United States tracked the evolution of mpox virus clade IIb and identified two separate introductions of clade Ib. The study, published on November 19, 2026, analyzed 326 high-quality whole-genome sequences from clinical specimens collected between August 2024 and June 2025. The surveillance, conducted across all ten United States Department of Health and Human Services regions by Labcorp, revealed twelve distinct circulating lineages of clade IIb and significant structural alterations, including large deletions near the inverted terminal repeats. Mutational analysis showed clear hallmarks of host-mediated editing by APOBEC3 cytidine deaminases, providing molecular evidence of sustained human-to-human transmission. The methodology, using Pacific Biosciences Sequel II technology with Molecular Loop probes, was validated through parallel metagenomic sequencing by the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention and custom computational simulations. The findings highlight the importance of genomic surveillance in understanding mpox virus evolution and preparedness for future outbreaks.

HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Monday, November 23, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Nationwide US Surveillance Tracks Mpox Clade IIb Evolution and Clade Ib Introductions by November 19, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 3 mins read 0 Nationwide US Surveillance Tracks Mpox Clade IIb Evolution and Clade Ib Introductions Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A nationwide genomic surveillance initiative across the United States has tracked the ongoing evolution of mpox virus, identifying two separate introductions of clade Ib alongside extensive genetic shifts in clade IIb. Researchers generated and analyzed 326 high-quality whole-genome sequences from residual clinical specimens collected between August 2024 and June 2025. The investigation captured significant patterns of viral adaptation, including characteristic human enzymatic mutation signatures and structural genomic deletions. The surveillance platform was established by building upon existing SARS-CoV-2 sequencing infrastructure to monitor mpox virus transmission dynamics following the 2022 to 2023 global outbreak. Clinical samples were obtained by Labcorp across all ten United States Department of Health and Human Services regions, providing comprehensive geographic coverage of circulating viral strains. This continuous nationwide sampling framework was designed to assess shifting lineage diversity and detect emerging genetic variants in clinical populations. To process residual mpox-positive clinical specimens, the investigators deployed a Molecular Loop probe-based long-read sequencing protocol utilizing Pacific Biosciences Sequel II technology. The long-read sequencing methodology generated complete genomic profiles, allowing researchers to evaluate both conserved core genomic regions and complex variable terminal ends. The assay demonstrated high technical reproducibility across biological replicates taken from individual patients during routine testing. Discover more Check Symptoms Read Med Journals Track Outbreaks Study Biology Read Security News Genomic characterization of the dataset identified two distinct clinical specimens containing clade Ib mpox virus, detected in January 2025 and June 2025. In addition to detecting clade Ib entries into the United States, the surveillance captured evolving patterns within clade IIb, revealing twelve distinct circulating lineages. Researchers also uncovered multiple structural alterations within clade IIb genomes, specifically large deletions ranging from approximately 1.6 to 17.6 kilobases positioned proximal to the inverted terminal repeats. Mutational analysis of the recovered viral sequences demonstrated clear hallmarks of host-mediated editing driven by APOBEC3 cytidine deaminases across both clade Ib and clade IIb samples. These specific mutation patterns provide substantial molecular evidence of sustained human-to-human transmission. Furthermore, the researchers detected significantly higher rates of APOBEC3-associated single nucleotide polymorphisms per kilobase in the variable regions of clade IIb genomes compared to the central conserved core region, with a statistical significance of P less than 0.001. Analytical accuracy of the probe-based sequencing approach was verified through parallel metagenomic sequencing conducted by the United States Centers for Disease Control and Prevention on selected clinical specimens. In addition, custom computational simulations confirmed that the long-read probe assay reliably discriminates between all known mpox virus clades and lineages, including lineages not previously documented in circulating United States samples. The authors concluded that the integrated nationwide surveillance architecture enables effective real-time tracking of pathogen outbreak evolution. Having established operational capacity for both SARS-CoV-2 and mpox virus, the genomic surveillance pipeline provides a validated technological foundation prepared for rapid deployment during potential future infectious disease emergence events. 随着猴痘的持续传播,自2022年以来美国成年人对该病毒的认识直线下降 - CIDRAP

As mpox continues to circulate, US adults’ awareness of the virus has plummeted since 2022 - CIDRAP
新闻简报CIDRAP 于 2026 年 11 月 19 日发布的新闻报道讨论了自 2022 年以来美国成年人对 猴痘病毒 的认识下降。报告指出,这种认识的下降可能会导致控制病毒的持续挑战。虽然文章没有提供具体的病例数、死亡人数或暴露数据,但它强调了公共卫生沟通和 疫苗接种 努力的重要性。报告建议,美国需要解决疫苗的犹豫,并提高对猴痘风险的理解,以防止进一步蔓延。猴痘 暴发 的状态正在调查中,文章没有详细说明当前的应对措施或其有效性。这些发现基于一项研究,该研究强调需要采取有针对性的公共卫生策略来对抗该病毒。

As mpox continues to circulate, US adults’ awareness of the virus has plummeted since 2022 - CIDRAP

News Brief
The news report from CIDRAP, published on November 19, 2026, discusses a decline in US adults' awareness of the mpox virus since 2022. The report notes that this reduced awareness may contribute to ongoing challenges in controlling the virus. The report suggests that the US needs to address vaccine hesitancy and improve understanding of mpox risks to prevent further spread. The status of the mpox outbreak is under investigation, and the article does not detail current response measures or their effectiveness. The findings are based on a study that highlights the need for targeted public health strategies to combat the virus.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

猴痘 30个月后重新出现

Mpox resurfaces after 30 months
新闻简报柬埔寨于 2026 年 11 月 23 日报告,确认了 30 多个月以来的首例新 猴痘 病例。该患者上周五检测呈阳性,目前正在医院接受 治疗 治疗。卫生部表示,这是时隔两年半后发现的首例病例,敦促公民保持警惕,防止出现聚集性或大规模的暴发。此前,柬埔寨在2023年12月至2024年5月期间记录了20例猴痘病例。猴痘病毒通过性接触、直接接触伤口、体液、唾液或受污染的材料传播,也可以从孕妇传播给胎儿。卫生部建议出现皮疹、发烧、淋巴结肿大或疲劳等症状的个人前往健康中心或医院咨询进行 猴痘 检测。回应包括正在进行的 治疗 和公共卫生建议,但尚未表明解决方案。
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026 年 11 月 23 日星期一,卫生部报告了失踪 30 个月后首例 猴痘 病例。该患者上周五(2026 年 11 月 20 日)检测结果呈阳性,目前正在医院接受 治疗 治疗。
地点与对象
该病例是在柬埔寨发现的,特别是在国家卫生系统的背景下。受影响人群包括柬埔寨公民,重点是那些可能通过性接触、直接接触伤口、体液、唾液或受污染材料而接触到病毒的人。
事件
柬埔寨在失踪 30 个月后确认了首例新的 猴痘 病例。卫生部报告了该病例,并敦促公民保持警惕,防止聚集性或大规模的暴发。
规模、影响与风险
这是自 2023 年 12 月以来柬埔寨首例 猴痘 病例。卫生部强调需要保持警惕,防止进一步传播,但没有提供具体的病例数、死亡人数或接触人数。该风险被描述为如果不采取预防措施,可能出现集群或大规模 暴发。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生部正在积极应对,敦促公民在出现症状时寻求咨询或检测。患者正在医院接受 治疗 治疗。该案件已得到确认,回应仍在进行中,尚未有解决的迹象。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox resurfaces after 30 months

News Brief
Cambodia has confirmed its first new mpox case in over 30 months, reported on 23 November 2026. The patient tested positive last Friday and is currently receiving treatment in a hospital. The Ministry of Health stated that this detection marks the first case after a two-and-a-half-year absence, urging citizens to remain vigilant to prevent clusters or large-scale outbreaks. Previously, Cambodia recorded 20 mpox cases between December 2023 and May 2024. The mpox virus spreads through sexual contact, direct contact with wounds, body fluids, saliva, or contaminated materials, and can also be transmitted from a pregnant woman to her fetus. The ministry advised individuals experiencing symptoms such as a blistering rash, fever, swollen lymph nodes, or fatigue to seek health centre or hospital consultation for mpox testing. The response includes ongoing treatment and public health advisories, with no indication of resolution yet.
Original source text (English)

The patient tested positive for the disease last Friday and is currently receiving treatment in a hospital, the statement said. Previously, Cambodia recorded 20 mpox cases between December 2023 and May 2024. “This new detection is the first case after Cambodia had not recorded any case of the disease for the past 30 months and six days,” the ministry stated, while urging all citizens to remain vigilant and protect their health and that of their families to prevent clusters or large-scale outbreaks. The mpox virus spreads from person to person through all forms of sexual contact, as well as direct contact with wounds, body fluids, saliva or contaminated materials. It can also be transmitted from a pregnant woman to her fetus via the umbilical cord during pregnancy or after birth. “Individuals experiencing symptoms such as a blistering rash on the face, palms, feet, body, eyes, mouth or genitals, along with fever, swollen lymph nodes, headache, muscle aches, lower back pain and fatigue should visit a health centre or hospital for consultation or mpox testing,” the statement advised. — Xinhua Follow us on our official WhatsApp channel for breaking news alerts and key updates! 柬埔寨在失踪 30 个月后确认首例新 猴痘 病例 - 星报

Cambodia confirms first new mpox case after 30-month absence - The Star
新闻简报根据卫生部的一份声明,柬埔寨确认了 30 多个月以来的首例新 猴痘 病例。该病例于周五发现,患者目前正在医院接受治疗治疗。猴痘病毒,也称为猴痘,通过性接触、直接接触伤口、体液、唾液或受污染的材料传播,并且可以从孕妇传播给胎儿。卫生部敦促公民保持警惕,保护自己的健康,防止聚集性或大规模的暴发。报告没有提供有关患者病情或感染源的更多详细信息。该信息基于官方声明,由于来自信誉良好的新闻来源,因此被认为是可靠的。该案是一个重大进展,因为它标志着 猴痘 在长期缺席后重返柬埔寨。
