这是时隔30个月后柬埔寨首例猴痘新病例。卫生部敦促公民保持警惕,防止聚集性或大规模的暴发。据描述,病毒的传播是通过各种形式的性接触、直接接触伤口、体液、唾液或受污染的材料而发生的,以及从孕妇在怀孕期间或出生后通过脐带传播给胎儿的。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Cambodia confirms first new mpox case after 30-month absence - The Star

News Brief
Cambodia has confirmed its first new mpox case in over 30 months, according to a statement from the Ministry of Health. The case was detected on Friday, and the patient is currently receiving treatment in a hospital. The mpox virus, also known as monkeypox, spreads through sexual contact, direct contact with wounds, body fluids, saliva, or contaminated materials, and can be transmitted from a pregnant woman to her fetus. The Ministry of Health has urged citizens to remain vigilant and protect their health to prevent clusters or large-scale outbreaks. The report does not provide further details about the patient's condition or the source of the infection. The information is based on official statements and is considered reliable as it comes from a reputable news source. The case is a significant development as it marks the return of mpox to Cambodia after a prolonged absence.
Original source text (English)

Cambodia 22h ago Cambodia showcases over 1,000 local products at major SME expo Cambodia has detected its first new case of mpox, also known as monkeypox, after the virus had been absent from the country for two and a half years. - World Health Organization PHNOM PENH: Cambodia has detected its first new case of mpox, also known as monkeypox, after the virus had been absent from the country for two and a half years, the Ministry of Health said in a statement on Sunday (Nov 22). The patient tested positive for the disease on Friday and is currently receiving treatment in a hospital, the statement said, without providing further details. Previously, Cambodia recorded 20 mpox cases between December 2023 and May 2024. "This new detection is the first case after Cambodia had not recorded any cases of the disease for the past 30 months and six days," the ministry stated. Advertisement Powered by: 00:00 00:00 Ad will close in:10 seconds orclick to close The ministry urged all citizens to remain vigilant and protect their health and that of their families to prevent clusters or large-scale outbreaks. According to the statement, the mpox virus spreads from person to person through all forms of sexual contact, as well as direct contact with wounds, body fluids, saliva, or contaminated materials. It can also be transmitted from a pregnant woman to her fetus via the umbilical cord during pregnancy or after birth. "Individuals experiencing symptoms such as a blistering rash on the face, palms, feet, body, eyes, mouth, or genitals, along with fever, swollen lymph nodes, headache, muscle aches, lower back pain and fatigue, should visit a health center or hospital for consultation or mpox testing," the statement advised. - Xinhua StarPicks CIMB OCTOFEST’S NATIONWIDE TOUR SUPPORTS LOCAL BUSINESSES Follow us on our official WhatsApp channel for breaking news alerts and key updates! 最近刚刚开始在全国传播的 猴痘 类型现已在丹佛都市区 - 哥伦比亚广播公司新闻

Type of mpox that just recently began spreading in the country is now in Denver metro area - CBS News
新闻简报据科罗拉多州公共卫生和环境部称,在丹佛都会区发现了 I 猴痘 分支的最新 暴发,它是 猴痘病毒 的变体。这种病毒以前被称为猴痘,已经在美国传播了四年,在转向 I 型之前,II 型病毒是主要毒株。CDPHE 强调,I 型 猴痘 通常会导致轻微病例,并且没有报告与此相关的死亡。他们敦促可能已经接触过病毒或正在出现肿块或皮疹等症状的个人考虑 疫苗接种 和测试。同样的 疫苗 Jynneos 对这两个分支都有效,卫生官员表示大多数人的风险“非常低”。此信息是正在进行的公共卫生应对措施的一部分,CDPHE 网站上提供了进一步的指导。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该报告于2026年11月19日发布。事件日期为2026年11月18日,当时科罗拉多州公共卫生和环境部宣布了进化枝I 猴痘的病例。
地点与对象
美国科罗拉多州丹佛都会区。受影响人群包括可能接触过进化枝 I 猴痘 并有感染风险的人。
事件
在丹佛都市区检测到了一种 猴痘,特别是进化枝 I 猴痘。科罗拉多州公共卫生和环境部证实了这一点。
规模、影响与风险
国内已发现200多例I型猴痘病例。进化枝 I 猴痘 尚未报告死亡。对于大多数人来说,这种风险被描述为“非常低”。
应对、状态与不确定性
科罗拉多州公共卫生和环境部鼓励高风险人群使用 疫苗接种。他们还建议有症状或可能接触过病毒的个人接受检测。应对工作正在进行中,并基于确诊病例。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Type of mpox that just recently began spreading in the country is now in Denver metro area - CBS News

News Brief
A recent outbreak of clade I mpox, a variant of the mpox virus, has been detected in the Denver metro area, according to the Colorado Department of Public Health and Environment. The virus, which was previously referred to as monkeypox, has been spreading in the United States for four years, with clade II being the dominant strain before the shift to clade I. The CDPHE has emphasized that clade I mpox typically results in mild cases and has reported no deaths associated with it. They are urging individuals who may have been exposed or are experiencing symptoms such as bumps or rashes to consider vaccination and testing. The same vaccine, Jynneos, is effective against both clades, and health officials have stated the risk for most people is "very low." This information is part of an ongoing public health response, with further guidance available on the CDPHE website.
Original source text (English)

Read Full Bio Jesse Sarles Updated on: November 18, 2026 / 8:59 PM MST / CBS Colorado Add CBS News on Google A type of mpox that has recently been spreading in the United States has now been detected in the Denver metro area. That's according to the Colorado Department of Public Health and Environment, which announced the case of clade I mpox on Wednesday and encouraged vaccination for people who are potentially at risk. "We strongly encourage those at increased risk for exposure to consider getting vaccinated against mpox. The same vaccine, Jynneos, protects against both clades," said state epidemiologist Dr. Rachel Herlihy in a prepared statement. There have been more than 200 cases of clade I mpox detected in the country. When concerns over the virus first began in the U.S. four years ago, the type that was spreading at that time was clade II, and health officials were referring to the virus as monkeypox. Near the end of 2022, the World Health Organization renamed the virus. Photo of an Mpox vaccination sign at a tent site at the Placer Pride festival event in Roseville, California, in 2024. Chris Allan / Getty Images The CDPHE says people who have been infected with clade I mpox this year in the U.S. have generally had mild cases. There have been no deaths reported from clade I mpox. Herlihy says the risk is "very low" for most people. State health officials said people who come down with bumps or a new rash in any part of their body, including their genitals, and who think they may have been exposed should get tested. Learn more about the virus at cdphe.colorado.gov/mpox. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

Clade I 猴痘 在科罗拉多州丹佛市确认 - Hoodline

Clade I Mpox Confirmed in Metro Denver, Colorado - Hoodline
新闻简报科罗拉多州卫生官员证实,丹佛都会区一名居民的 I 型 猴痘 检测结果呈阳性。该病例是美国 I 型 猴痘 病例不断增长趋势的一部分,截至 2026 年 10 月,至少 31 个州和波多黎各报告了 200 多例病例。CDC 指出,尽管官员们敦促高风险个体,特别是男男性行为者,考虑 疫苗接种,但公众面临的风险仍然较低。JYNNEOS 疫苗 可防御进化枝 I 和进化枝 II 猴痘。虽然历史上认为 I 型分支更为严重,但最近美国的病例相对较轻,没有死亡报告。官员强调,没有身体接触的短暂互动不太可能传播病毒,重点是通过疫苗接种和公共卫生措施预防传播。

科罗拉多州卫生官员证实,一名丹佛都市区居民的 I 猴痘 分支检测呈阳性。该病例是美国 I 类 猴痘 病例不断增长的趋势的一部分,但没有死亡报告。
规模、影响与风险
截至 10 月,CDC 报告了至少 31 个州和波多黎各的 200 多例 I 型 猴痘 病例。公众面临的风险仍然很低,但官员们敦促高风险个人考虑 疫苗接种。
应对、状态与不确定性
科罗拉多州卫生官员证实了这一病例,并鼓励高风险人群使用 疫苗接种。疾病预防控制中心指出,普通公众的风险较低,男男性行为者的风险为低至中度。

Clade I Mpox Confirmed in Metro Denver, Colorado - Hoodline

News Brief
A metro Denver resident has tested positive for clade I mpox, confirmed by Colorado state health officials. This case is part of a growing trend of clade I mpox cases in the U.S., with over 200 cases reported across at least 31 states and Puerto Rico as of October 2026. The CDC has noted that the risk to the general public remains low, though officials are urging higher-risk individuals, particularly men who have sex with men, to consider vaccination. The JYNNEOS vaccine protects against both clade I and clade II mpox. While clade I has been historically considered more severe, recent U.S. cases have been relatively mild with no reported deaths. Officials emphasize that brief interactions without physical contact are unlikely to spread the virus, and the focus is on preventing transmission through vaccination and public health measures.

Subscribe Des Moines 38°F +46 +46 +46 +45 +45 +45 +45 +43 +41 +41 +42 +43 +44 +45 +45 +44 +43 +40 +39 +38 +37 +36 +35 +34 Sun 46°F 34°F +33 +33 +32 +31 +30 +30 +29 +29 +29 +32 +34 +36 +37 +37 +38 +37 +36 +33 +31 +30 +29 +29 +28 +28 Mon 38°F 28°F +27 +27 +26 +26 +25 +25 +25 +25 +26 +28 +30 +32 +34 +35 +36 +36 +35 +32 +30 +29 +29 +28 +29 +29 Tue 36°F 25°F +29 +29 +29 +29 +29 +29 +29 +29 +31 +31 +32 +33 +34 +34 +34 +34 +34 +33 +33 +33 +33 +32 +32 +32 Wed 34°F 29°F +31 +31 +31 +31 +31 +30 +30 +31 +32 +33 +34 +34 +35 +35 +35 +35 +35 +35 +34 +33 +33 +32 +31 +30 Thu 35°F 30°F +29 +29 +29 +29 +29 +29 +29 +31 +32 +34 +36 +38 +39 +39 +39 +39 +38 +38 +38 +37 +37 +36 +36 +35 Fri 39°F 29°F +35 +34 +33 +32 +31 +30 +29 +31 +34 +37 +39 +42 +44 +44 +43 +43 +42 +42 +41 +40 +39 +38 +37 +35 Sat 44°F 29°F +34 +35 +35 +36 +37 +37 +38 +39 +40 +42 +43 +44 +46 +45 +45 +45 +44 +44 +43 +42 +42 +41 +40 +39 +38 +38 +38 +38 +38 +38 +38 +39 +40 +40 +41 +42 +42 +42 +40 +40 +38 +38 +37 +36 +35 +34 +33 +32 +31 +31 +31 +31 +31 +31 +32 +33 +35 +37 +38 +40 +42 +42 +42 +42 +42 +42 +38 +38 +36 +35 +34 +34 More forecasts: Atlanta 30 day forecast Denver/ Science, Tech & Medicine Metro Denver Resident Tests Positive for Clade I Mpox, Colorado Urges Vaccination By Hailey Armstrong Published on November 19, 2026 Colorado Department of Public Health and Environment — State Mpox Case Confirmation Google Street View A metro Denver resident has tested positive for clade I mpox, Colorado state health officials confirmed, as the state joins a growing list of jurisdictions reporting the more closely watched strain of the virus. Officials are now urging people at higher risk of exposure to consider getting vaccinated, even as they stress that danger to the general public remains very low. What Colorado Officials Are Saying State epidemiologist Rachel Herlihy said the risk to the general population remains very low despite clade I mpox being detected in Colorado, and she encouraged people at increased risk for exposure to consider getting vaccinated against mpox. Herlihy also noted that the JYNNEOS vaccine protects against both mpox clades, according to 9News. Colorado state health officials have confirmed the case in a metro Denver resident and are encouraging people at higher risk to consider vaccination. Clade I is one of two main types of virus that cause mpox, distinct from clade II, which caused the global mpox outbreak that began in 2022 and continues to circulate. Public health officials are tracking clade I closely because it has only recently begun spreading within the United States, and the Colorado Department of Public Health and Environment says it has historically been considered more severe than clade II. Even so, recent U.S. clade I cases have been relatively mild with no reported deaths. A National Trend of Domestic Spread The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention has reported more than 200 clade I mpox cases across at least 31 states and Puerto Rico as of October, with the agency noting that some people in the United States have likely acquired clade I mpox domestically without a known connection to international travel. That national picture has been building for months. As of its October 5 update, the CDC put the national clade I case count at 80, according to Medscape, and by October 14 that figure had climbed past 90 cases in at least 23 states and Puerto Rico, per Medical Daily. The CDC has said cases with presumed domestic transmission had been reported in more than 10 jurisdictions nationwide, a pattern the agency says points toward a likely transition to sustained clade Ib transmission in the United States, as reported by Medical Daily. Globally, more than 300 clade Ib cases have been diagnosed in high-income countries without a single reported death, the same Medscape report notes. The CDC's own risk assessment has held steady: low for the general public, and low to moderate for men who have sex with men. Similar Cases Surfacing Elsewhere in the West Colorado is not the only state watching clade I activity turn up without obvious travel links. In California's Stanislaus County, health officials identified four recent mpox cases, and state officials confirmed two of them were clade I; three of the four patients had not traveled outside the country, the same outlet reports. That echoes a pattern seen in California roughly a year earlier, when the state confirmed three unrelated clade I cases in Long Beach and Los Angeles County among people with no international travel and no contact with one another. Who Is Considered Higher Risk, and Why Vaccination Matters Anyone can get mpox through close physical contact with someone who has it, according to CDPHE, since the virus spreads mainly through close physical contact, including sex, or indirect contact with an infected person's sores, bumps, or lesions. Brief interactions without physical contact are unlikely to spread mpox, the agency says. Men who have sex with men have been at higher risk for contracting mpox, and transgender women and gender-diverse people have also been disproportionately affected, per CDPHE. State health officials describe mpox vaccination as the best way to prevent the disease. Physicians elsewhere have echoed that message in clinical settings: infectious disease specialist Jason Zucker has advised doctors to use every office visit to offer and complete the two-dose JYNNEOS vaccine series for eligible at-risk patients, according to Medscape's report. In Stanislaus County, public health officials similarly recommended the two-dose JYNNEOS vaccine for several higher-risk groups, including gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men, Medical Daily reported. Recognizing Mpox Symptoms CDPHE lists mpox symptoms as including a new rash or bumps on the hands, feet, chest, face, mouth, near the genitals, or in the rectum, often alongside flu-like symptoms such as fever, chills, headache, fatigue, and muscle aches. The rash can resemble pimples or blisters and may be painful or itchy, and it typically goes through several stages, including scabs, before healing, the agency says. People who develop a new rash or bumps and think they may have been exposed should get tested for mpox even if they have been vaccinated or previously infected, according to CDPHE. 调查:大多数美国人仍然不了解 猴痘、疫苗 和 传播 风险

Survey: Most Americans remain unaware of mpox vaccine and transmission risks
新闻简报宾夕法尼亚大学安纳伯格公共政策中心 (APPC) 于 2026 年 11 月发布的一项调查显示,大多数美国人仍然不了解 猴痘 及其 传播 风险。这项涵盖 1,842 名美国成年人的调查发现,只有 24% 的人对 猴痘 有一定程度或非常熟悉,低于 2024 年 7 月的 23%。对 猴痘 疫苗 的认知度也很低,44% 的人知道它的存在,与 2024 年相比没有变化。CDC 报告称,有 1,369 名 猴痘 2026 年的病例数,表明持续的低水平 传播。尽管了解有限,但 69% 的受访者表示,如果接触到疫苗,他们可能会接种疫苗,其中 44% 的人表示可能性很大。CDC 强调,猴痘 疫苗 可以预防疾病,并且可以在暴露后施用。然而,调查强调了公众持续存在的误解,例如 16% 的人认为 猴痘 是生物工程的,7% 的人错误地将 Covid-19 疫苗 与 猴痘 风险增加联系起来。这些发现强调需要改进关于 猴痘、其 传播 和 疫苗接种 的公众教育。
查看新闻五要素
时间
在全球重大 暴发 和 猴痘 事件发生四年后,美国的病毒性疾病病例仍持续处于低水平。该调查于 2026 年 10 月结束,数据收集于 2026 年 9 月 5 日,调查对象为 1,842 名美国受访者。
地点与对象
美国,特别关注美国成年人,因为该调查是在全国范围内进行的。
事件
宾夕法尼亚大学安纳伯格公共政策中心(APPC)进行的一项调查发现,公众对 猴痘 的认识和了解仍然很低,只有 24% 的美国成年人对 猴痘 有所了解或非常熟悉,44% 的人知道 疫苗 的存在。CDC 报告 2026 年有 1,369 例 猴痘 病例,表明 猴痘 持续处于低水平。
规模、影响与风险
调查凸显了持续存在的知识空白,76%的美国成年人对猴痘不太熟悉或完全不熟悉。仅有18%的人正确识别猴痘的传染性低于新冠(Covid-19),33%的人知道男男性行为者感染风险更高。CDC指出,猴痘已转向地方性传播,且仍有持续的低水平传播。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 表示,猴痘 疫苗 预计可以预防这种疾病,并且可以在暴露后施用。然而,调查表明,公众对 传播、风险和 疫苗接种 的了解仍然有限,自 2024 年以来,认识没有显着变化。CDC 建议服用两剂 JYNNEOS 疫苗 以获得最大程度的保护。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Survey: Most Americans remain unaware of mpox vaccine and transmission risks

News Brief
A survey conducted by the Annenberg Public Policy Center (APPC) of the University of Pennsylvania, published in November 2026, reveals that most Americans remain unaware of mpox and its transmission risks. The survey, which included 1,842 U.S. adults, found that only 24% are somewhat or very familiar with mpox, down from 23% in July 2024. Awareness of the mpox vaccine is also low, with 44% knowing it exists, unchanged from 2024. The CDC reported 1,369 mpox cases in 2026, indicating ongoing low-level transmission. Despite limited knowledge, 69% of respondents said they would be likely to get vaccinated if exposed, with 44% expressing a strong likelihood. The CDC emphasizes that mpox vaccines can protect against the disease and may be administered post-exposure. However, the survey highlights persistent public misconceptions, such as 16% believing mpox was bioengineered, and 7% incorrectly associating the Covid-19 vaccine with increased mpox risk. These findings underscore the need for improved public education on mpox, its transmission, and vaccination.
Original source text (English)
Four years after a major global outbreak of mpox, the United States continues to see a persistent, low level of cases of the viral disease. But Americans' awareness and knowledge of mpox have changed little over the past couple of years, according to a new nationally representative survey conducted by the Annenberg Public Policy Center (APPC) of the University of Pennsylvania. In the survey, which concluded in October, just 8% of U.S. adults say they have seen, read, or heard anything about mpox in the past month, down from 11% in July 2024 and dramatically down from 80% in July 2022, when the global mpox outbreak was gaining widespread attention. The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) says that since 2022 "cases have declined substantially in the United States; however, ongoing low-level transmission has persisted," indicating a likely transition toward endemic circulation. As of Oct. 24, the CDC says 1,369 cases of mpox, also called monkeypox, have been reported in 2026. The CDC says mpox is transmitted person-to-person by close (including intimate or sexual) contact with the rashes, scabs or bodily fluids of someone who has monkeypox; contact with contaminated materials, including clothing, bedding, towels, and dishes; or contact with small wild animals in West and Central Africa. The Annenberg Science and Public Health (ASAPH) survey conducted Sept. 22-Oct. 5, 2026, among 1,842 empaneled U.S. adults, finds that many of the knowledge gaps identified in our 2024 mpox survey remain essentially unchanged. The 2024 survey found that public knowledge about mpox had fallen substantially from the heightened awareness during the 2022 outbreak. Findings Among the findings: Familiarity remains low: Just 24% say they are somewhat or very familiar with mpox, statistically unchanged from 23% in July 2024. Three-quarters (76%) say they are not too familiar or not at all familiar with it. Just 24% say they are somewhat or very familiar with mpox, statistically unchanged from 23% in July 2024. Three-quarters (76%) say they are not too familiar or not at all familiar with it. Few worry about contracting it: Just 7% say they are somewhat or very worried about contracting mpox in the next three months, statistically unchanged from 5% in 2024 and far below the 21% who were worried in August 2022. Just 7% say they are somewhat or very worried about contracting mpox in the next three months, statistically unchanged from 5% in 2024 and far below the 21% who were worried in August 2022. Knowledge about a vaccine has not improved: Less than half (44%) know that a vaccine against mpox exists, virtually unchanged from 45% in 2024 and below 61% in August 2022. Nearly half (48%) are unsure whether a vaccine exists. Less than half (44%) know that a vaccine against mpox exists, virtually unchanged from 45% in 2024 and below 61% in August 2022. Nearly half (48%) are unsure whether a vaccine exists. Most unsure about mpox's contagiousness relative to Covid-19: Only 18% correctly say mpox is less contagious than Covid-19, statistically unchanged from 17% in 2024. Nearly two-thirds (62%) are unsure. In August 2022, 41% knew mpox was less contagious. Only 18% correctly say mpox is less contagious than Covid-19, statistically unchanged from 17% in 2024. Nearly two-thirds (62%) are unsure. In August 2022, 41% knew mpox was less contagious. Knowledge about a group at elevated risk remains low: 33% know that men who have sex with men are at higher risk of mpox infection, unchanged from 34% in 2024 and about half the 63% who knew this in August 2022. More than half (53%) are unsure. 33% know that men who have sex with men are at higher risk of mpox infection, unchanged from 34% in 2024 and about half the 63% who knew this in August 2022. More than half (53%) are unsure. Knowledge of transmission modes is unchanged: 49% know that sharing bedding, clothing, or towels used by someone with mpox presents a higher risk of infection, compared with 48% in 2024. And 51% recognize the risk associated with close face-to-face contact with an infected person, compared with 49% in 2024. "The persistence of these knowledge gaps matters because the virus has not stood still," said Ken Winneg, APPC's managing director of survey research. "As the epidemiology of mpox changes, public understanding of who is at risk, how the virus spreads, and how vaccination can protect against it needs to keep pace." Some signs of increased knowledge Although most measures changed little from 2024, the survey finds improvement in two areas: Nearly 3 in 10 Americans (29%) know that the mpox virus can spread through the placenta of a pregnant person who has mpox, an increase from 21% in July 2024. Still, nearly two-thirds (63%) are unsure. The CDC says monkeypox virus can be transmitted to a fetus during pregnancy, potentially posing risks to both the mother and newborn. Three-quarters (73%) know that people with mpox should isolate at home until the rash is gone, up from 68% in 2024. The CDC advises people with mpox to isolate until the rash has fully healed, the scabs have fallen off, and a fresh layer of intact skin has formed. Willingness to get vaccinated remains high Despite limited knowledge about mpox, most Americans say they would be likely to get vaccinated if exposed to the virus. In the survey, 69% say they would be "somewhat" or "very" likely to get an mpox vaccine if exposed, virtually unchanged from 68% in 2024. Another 2% say they are already vaccinated. The proportion saying they would be "very" likely to get vaccinated increased significantly, however, from 38% in 2024 to 44% today. Nearly three-quarters of Americans (73%) say the benefits of mpox vaccines outweigh their risks, compared with 70% in 2024, a difference that is not statistically significant. The CDC recommends two doses of the JYNNEOS vaccine for maximum protection for people for whom mpox vaccination is recommended. The CDC says mpox vaccines are expected to protect against the disease and can also be administered after exposure to help prevent illness. Misperceptions and uncertainty persist The survey finds little change in beliefs about two unsupported claims involving mpox. Sixteen percent say it is definitely or probably true that mpox was bioengineered in a laboratory, for which there is no evidence, statistically unchanged from 15% in 2024. Another 41% are unsure, while 43% correctly say the claim is probably or definitely false. According to FactCheck.org, a project of the Annenberg Public Policy Center, this claim has been debunked. Seven percent incorrectly say that receiving the Covid-19 vaccine increases a person's chances of getting mpox, statistically unchanged from 6% in 2024. Nearly two-thirds (63%) correctly reject the claim, compared with 58% in 2024, while 30% are unsure. FactCheck.org labels this a false claim. About the survey The latest wave of the Annenberg Science and Public Health (ASAPH) survey, Wave 31, was conducted for APPC from Sept. 22-Oct. 5, 2026, among 1,842 U.S. adults by SSRS, an independent research company. Interviews were conducted by web and telephone among a nationally representative probability sample drawn from the SSRS Opinion Panel, with the data weighted to represent the U.S. adult population. The margin of error is ±3.2 percentage points. The Annenberg Public Policy Center has been tracking Americans' knowledge, beliefs, and behaviors regarding vaccination, Covid-19, flu, RSV, and other consequential health issues through its ASAPH survey and other national samples since April 2021. In addition to Winneg, the survey team includes Patrick E. Jamieson, director of the Annenberg Health and Risk Communication Institute, which oversees the survey; research analyst Laura A. Gibson; and Kathleen Hall Jamieson, APPC's director emerita

The CDC reported 1,369 mpox cases in 2026, indicating ongoing low-level transmission.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

Global Mpox Prevalence Among Men Who Have Sex With Men Reached 2.30 Percent - Bioengineer.org

Global Mpox Prevalence Among Men Who Have Sex With Men Reached 2.30 Percent - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报A comprehensive systematic review and meta-analysis published in the journal Infectious Diseases of Poverty has established that the pooled prevalence of mpox infection among men who have sex with men (MSM) worldwide was 2.30 percent during the multi-country outbreak period. The study, led by researchers Luqiu Tao, Yanzheng Zou, Lilei Wang, and Wei Wang, evaluated 28 investigations involving 46,598 participants across 15 countries, with 1,274 confirmed mpox cases. The prevalence varied significantly, ranging from 0.00 to 16.31 percent. The analysis used a random-effects model and Freeman-Tukey double arcsine transformation to normalize data, with a 95% confidence interval of 1.29 to 3.57 percent. The World Health Organization declared the outbreak a Public Health Emergency of International Concern in July 2022 due to its rapid spread across non-endemic regions. The study followed PRISMA guidelines and was registered on PROSPERO under CRD42024587173, but it acknowledged limitations in explaining residual heterogeneity, suggesting that local sexual network configurations and community stigma levels were not fully captured in the analysis.
查看新闻五要素
时间
The study was published on November 19, 2026, and the data spanned the period from 2022 to 2024. The pooled prevalence of 2.30 percent was calculated based on data from the multi-country outbreak period.
地点与对象
该研究评估了来自 15 个国家的 28 项调查,包括欧洲(奥地利、比利时、丹麦、法国、德国、爱尔兰、荷兰、西班牙、英国)、亚洲(中国、日本)、北美(加拿大、以色列)和南美洲(巴西)。研究对象为男男性行为者 (MSM) 社区,共有 46,598 名参与者和 1,274 例确诊的 猴痘 病例。
事件
A comprehensive systematic review and meta-analysis published in the journal Infectious Diseases of Poverty reported that the pooled prevalence of mpox infection among men who have sex with men worldwide was 2.30 percent during the multi-country outbreak period. B routine priorityPublic-health news

Global Mpox Prevalence Among Men Who Have Sex With Men Reached 2.30 Percent - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A comprehensive systematic review and meta-analysis published in the journal Infectious Diseases of Poverty has established that the pooled prevalence of mpox infection among men who have sex with men (MSM) worldwide was 2.30 percent during the multi-country outbreak period. The study, led by researchers Luqiu Tao, Yanzheng Zou, Lilei Wang, and Wei Wang, evaluated 28 investigations involving 46,598 participants across 15 countries, with 1,274 confirmed mpox cases. The prevalence varied significantly, ranging from 0.00 to 16.31 percent. The analysis used a random-effects model and Freeman-Tukey double arcsine transformation to normalize data, with a 95% confidence interval of 1.29 to 3.57 percent. The World Health Organization declared the outbreak a Public Health Emergency of International Concern in July 2022 due to its rapid spread across non-endemic regions. The study followed PRISMA guidelines and was registered on PROSPERO under CRD42024587173, but it acknowledged limitations in explaining residual heterogeneity, suggesting that local sexual network configurations and community stigma levels were not fully captured in the analysis.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Monday, November 23, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Global Mpox Prevalence Among Men Who Have Sex With Men Reached 2.30 Percent by November 19, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 7 mins read 0 Global Mpox Prevalence Among Men Who Have Sex With Men Reached 2.30 Percent Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A comprehensive systematic review and meta-analysis published in the journal Infectious Diseases of Poverty has established that the pooled prevalence of mpox infection among men who have sex with men worldwide was 2.30 percent during the multi-country outbreak period. Led by researchers Luqiu Tao, Yanzheng Zou, Lilei Wang, and Wei Wang, the global research synthesis evaluated twenty-eight eligible investigations comprising 46,598 participants from men who have sex with men communities and 1,274 confirmed mpox cases spanning fifteen countries. Across the individual primary studies, observed infection proportions varied substantially, spanning from 0.00 percent to a peak of 16.31 percent. The authors performed quantitative pooling using a random-effects statistical model after detecting significant between-study heterogeneity, with an I-squared statistic of 97.3 percent, a tau-squared of 0.0091, and a Cochran Q test yielding a p-value below 0.0001. Applying the Freeman-Tukey double arcsine transformation to normalize proportional data, the researchers calculated an overall pooled mpox infection prevalence of 2.30 percent, bounded by a ninety-five percent confidence interval ranging from 1.29 percent to 3.57 percent. Normality of these transformed proportions was confirmed using the Shapiro-Wilk test, which returned a statistic of 0.936 and a p-value of 0.087, verifying that the aggregated analytical framework adhered to standard statistical assumptions while capturing the overall disease burden across international peer-reviewed literature. The historical and epidemiological context supporting this investigation traces back to the emergence of mpox, an infectious zoonotic condition caused by the monkeypox virus that was first identified in a human patient in the Democratic Republic of the Congo in 1970. Although the virus historically caused sporadic endemic outbreaks largely confined to forested zones of central and western Africa, the epidemiological presentation transformed radically during the 2022 to 2024 global resurgence. During this event, sustained human-to-human transmission was predominantly sustained through sexual contact within men who have sex with men networks, driven primarily by the Clade IIb viral lineage. The World Health Organization declared the multi-country spread a Public Health Emergency of International Concern in July 2022 after infections expanded rapidly across non-endemic territories in Europe and North America through close physical and mucosal contact. Although Clade IIb generally produces milder clinical illness and lower fatality than historical Clade I lineages, it still resulted in severe complications, painful mucosal lesions, and widespread hospitalizations. A second international emergency was subsequently declared in August 2024 following the surge and cross-border dissemination of the more transmissible Clade Ib lineage originating from Central Africa, which coincided with the re-emergence of Clade IIb cases in Nigeria and South Africa. Discover more Get Tested Browse Magazines men To rigorously aggregate published evidence regarding mpox burdens in this heavily affected demographic, the research team designed their systematic review in accordance with the Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses guidelines and formally registered the analytical protocol on the PROSPERO international database under registration number CRD42024587173. The investigators performed exhaustive literature searches across five prominent medical and scientific databases, namely PubMed, Web of Science, Embase, the China National Knowledge Infrastructure, and Wanfang Data, spanning from database inception through December 16, 2024. Search queries combined established medical subject headings and free-text terms covering monkeypox virus designations alongside terminology identifying gay, bisexual, and other men who have sex with men networks. The inclusion criteria required that studies be published in English or Chinese, provide explicit numbers of mpox cases and overall sample denominators among men who have sex with men, establish confirmed diagnoses through either laboratory testing or formal self-report, and capture observational periods following the introduction of initial cases. Non-primary literature including narrative reviews, isolated case reports, and studies lacking explicit numerator or denominator data were excluded during screening. Discover more Check Symptoms Read Med Journals Track Fertility Support Pride The study selection process initiated with 2,414 retrieved records, from which duplicate entries were eliminated, leaving 1,606 unique publications to undergo initial title and abstract screening. Of these, 1,479 non-pertinent reports were set aside, allowing 127 articles to advance to full-text assessment. Following rigorous evaluation against predefined exclusion criteria, ninety-nine records were eliminated, leaving twenty-eight primary studies published between 2022 and 2024 that satisfied all methodological conditions for quantitative synthesis. Methodological quality was evaluated using validated instruments tailored to observational research designs, specifically utilizing the American Agency for Healthcare Research and Quality eleven-item tool for cross-sectional studies and the Newcastle-Ottawa Scale for cohort and case-control formats. Across the twenty-eight analyzed investigations, twenty-three studies met the criteria for moderate methodological quality, encompassing twenty cross-sectional papers and three longitudinal or case-control reports, while five cross-sectional publications achieved high quality scores. Sensitivity analysis restricted strictly to the five high-quality investigations yielded a pooled prevalence of 1.91 percent with a ninety-five percent confidence interval from 0.49 to 4.20 percent, which remained statistically comparable to the overall cohort finding of 2.30 percent. Geographic and participant recruitment characteristics varied considerably across the final collection of twenty-eight publications, illustrating diverse research settings across fifteen countries. Europe accounted for the largest volume of publications with sixteen investigations, spanning Austria, Belgium, Denmark, France, Germany, Ireland, the Netherlands, Spain, and the United Kingdom. Asia contributed seven studies from China and Japan, alongside single investigations from Canada and Israel, while North America supplied three studies and South America provided two investigations, both based in Brazil. Twenty-one investigations were conducted in nations classified as high-income economies by the World Bank, whereas the remaining seven studies took place in upper-middle-income countries. Regarding recruitment venues, twelve studies enrolled individuals visiting dedicated sexual health clinics, nine engaged participants through online social media platforms, three examined pre-existing cohorts related to men who have sex with men, and four utilized mixed recruitment channels. Notably, all twenty-eight included studies employed non-probability sampling methods: twenty-three relied on convenience sampling, three used purposive sampling, and two utilized combined convenience and snowball recruitment approaches to reach eligible study participants. Subgroup analyses stratified by continental geography uncovered striking spatial disparities in infection burden that represented the primary source of variance across the international literature. Men who have sex with men surveyed in South America exhibited a pooled mpox prevalence of 9.58 percent, with a ninety-five percent confidence interval between 2.71 and 20.01 percent, which was substantially higher than the 2.40 percent observed in Europe, the 1.87 percent recorded in North America, and the 1.14 percent reported across Asian studies. The absolute risk difference between South America and Asia reached 8.4 percent, with a ninety-five percent confidence interval of 7.7 to 9.2 percent, translating to an excess burden of eighty-four cases per 1,000 individuals. Absolute risk differences between South America and Europe reached 7.2 percent, while Europe compared to Asia displayed an absolute difference of 1.3 percent. In univariable meta-regression modeling, geographic continent emerged as the single largest contributor to study heterogeneity, accounting for 22.24 percent of between-study variance with a statistically significant p-value of 0.02. Primary sampling methodology also explained 10.69 percent of variance in univariable testing, whereas economic classification, diagnostic criteria, and study quality scores showed no meaningful statistical associations. Multivariable meta-regression confirmed that continental setting remained an independent predictor of mpox prevalence even after statistically adjusting for participant sampling methodologies. The multivariable regression model demonstrated overall significance, yielding an omnibus test statistic of 12.53 across five degrees of freedom with a p-value of 0.028, jointly accounting for 24.81 percent of the total between-study heterogeneity. Within this adjusted framework, the elevated prevalence in South America relative to Asia retained statistical significance, exhibiting a regression coefficient beta of 0.168, a ninety-five percent confidence interval from 0.028 to 0.309, and a p-value of 0.019. In contrast, specific sampling modalities did not maintain independent statistical significance in the adjusted multivariable model. The researchers noted that while regional prevalence differed sharply, other analyzed covariates exhibited minimal divergence. Subgroup comparisons across epidemic timing demonstrated that studies carried out in 2022 showed a pooled prevalence of 2.89 percent compared to 1.21 percent in investigations conducted during 2023, reflecting global outbreak deceleration, although this temporal difference did not reach formal statistical significance with a p-value of 0.06. Counterintuitively, subgroup stratifications based on perceived behavioral or clinical vulnerability revealed no statistically significant differences in mpox prevalence when compared against broader cohorts. Ten studies specifically examined high-risk populations, categorized by human immunodeficiency virus infection, pre-exposure prophylaxis utilization, concurrent sexually transmitted infections, or active high-risk sexual practices such as chemsex or multiple concurrent sexual partners. Within these high-risk groups, the pooled prevalence was 1.88 percent, with a ninety-five percent confidence interval of 0.22 to 4.73 percent, which was slightly lower than the 2.50 percent observed in general community recruitment, exhibiting no statistically significant variance with a p-value of 0.79 and an absolute risk difference of negative 0.62 percent. Similarly, eight studies evaluated asymptomatic participants who reported no mpox-compatible symptoms at the time of screening; this asymptomatic group showed an infection prevalence of 1.28 percent, ranging from 0.10 to 3.33 percent, compared to 2.71 percent among general study groups, with a non-significant p-value of 0.34. Clinical recruitment venues also yielded a modest 1.40 percent prevalence compared to 3.68 percent via online networks and 4.36 percent within established research cohorts. The authors discussed several epidemiological and behavioral dynamics that could account for why clinically designated high-risk groups did not exhibit higher infection rates in cross-sectional testing. They highlighted the prevention paradox, wherein individuals recognized as high risk often maintain deeper engagement with dedicated sexual health clinics and specialized care infrastructure. In the analyzed dataset, seven of the ten studies investigating high-risk groups were situated in countries with established mpox vaccine availability, and participants were predominantly drawn from clinical cohorts with routine medical linkages. Frequent healthcare interaction fosters elevated health literacy, regular diagnostic screening, and rapid access to preventive interventions, including smallpox-derived vaccines such as ACAM2000, MVA-BN, LC16m8, and OrthopoxVac. This proactive engagement likely facilitated earlier diagnosis and prompt isolation, thereby reducing point prevalence during cross-sectional surveys. Conversely, community and online recruitment across general populations captured broader, less clinically connected networks where unrecognized or milder infections could persist undetected before formal assessment. The synthesis also addressed methodological challenges regarding asymptomatic transmission and diagnostic criteria. Across fifteen studies confirming mpox through laboratory diagnostics such as polymerase chain reaction or serological assays, pooled prevalence was 2.24 percent, which was indistinguishable from the overall 2.30 percent estimate that included thirteen studies relying on verified self-reported diagnoses, yielding a subgroup comparison p-value of 0.96. Addressing asymptomatic cohorts, the authors noted that single-time-point cross-sectional surveys inherently struggle to differentiate true asymptomatic infection from pre-symptomatic viral shedding, as multiple participants in cited screening trials subsequently developed clinical manifestations after their initial positive test. Funnel plot symmetry, supported by Egger regression testing with a p-value of 0.57 and Begg rank correlation with a p-value of 0.68, indicated an absence of notable publication bias. Furthermore, leave-one-out sensitivity testing confirmed that no single primary study exerted disproportionate leverage over the pooled results. Despite the robustness of the quantitative synthesis, the authors underscored critical limitations inherent to the underlying evidence base. First, the ecological nature of aggregated meta-regression precludes inferring individual-level risk factors from population-level continental associations. Second, the multivariable model left substantial residual heterogeneity unexplained, indicating that local sexual network configurations, precise community stigma levels, and micro-epidemic curves remained unmeasured. Third, geographic coverage was notably unbalanced, with South American findings originating exclusively from two Brazilian investigations and no studies identified from the African continent or Oceania. Fourth, primary studies lacked systematic reporting of viral clades, meaning the synthesis predominantly reflects the 2022 to 2024 Clade IIb pandemic rather than the evolving dynamics of Clade Ib. Finally, HIV clinical markers such as CD4 counts and viral loads were inconsistently documented across primary cohorts. The authors concluded that future outbreak management requires geographically prioritized resource allocation and standardized surveillance metrics to monitor shifting viral transmission patterns effectively. 随着猴痘的持续传播,2022年以来美国成年人对该病毒的认知度直线下降

As mpox continues to circulate, US adults’ awareness of the virus has plummeted since 2022
新闻简报宾夕法尼亚大学安纳伯格公共政策中心(APPC)的一项调查显示,自 2022 年以来,美国成年人对 猴痘 的认知度显着下降。在 2026 年 9 月 22 日至 10 月 5 日进行的最新调查中,1,842 名成年人中只有 8% 表示在过去一个月中对 猴痘 的认知度下降,低于 2024 年 7 月的 11%,比 2026 年 7 月低 80%。2022年的水平。CDC 指出,猴痘 已转变为地方性流行,且 猴痘 持续处于低水平。尽管如此,调查还是显示了一些进展,例如 73% 的受访者知道 猴痘 患者应该隔离,直到皮疹消失。然而,知识差距仍然存在,只有 18% 的人正确识别出 猴痘 的传染性低于 COVID-19。有关该病毒的错误信息,包括有关其起源和 传播 的说法仍然普遍存在,这凸显了改善公众教育的必要性。

时间
该调查于2026年9月22日至10月5日进行,并与2024年7月和2022年8月进行了比较。
地点与对象
该调查针对美国成年人,共有 1,842 名参与者接受调查。
事件
宾夕法尼亚大学安纳伯格公共政策中心(APPC)进行的一项调查发现,自 2022 年以来,美国成年人对 猴痘 的认知度显着下降,过去一个月只有 8% 的人表示知晓。
规模、影响与风险
对 猴痘 的认知度从 2024 年 7 月的 11% 下降到最近一次调查的 8%,自 2022 年 7 月以来下降了 80%。公众对 猴痘、传播、风险因素和 疫苗接种 的了解仍然有限,只有 49% 的人知道它是如何传播的,18% 的人正确地将其识别为比 COVID-19 的传染性低。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 指出,猴痘 已转变为地方性流行,且 猴痘 持续处于低水平。该调查强调了持续存在的知识差距和错误信息,但也显示了一些理解有所增强的迹象,例如 73% 的人知道 猴痘 患者应该隔离,直到皮疹消失。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

As mpox continues to circulate, US adults’ awareness of the virus has plummeted since 2022

News Brief
A survey by the Annenberg Public Policy Center (APPC) at the University of Pennsylvania reveals that awareness of mpox among US adults has significantly declined since 2022. In the most recent survey, conducted from September 22 to October 5, 2026, only 8% of 1,842 adults reported awareness of mpox in the past month, down from 11% in July 2024 and 80% lower than the 2022 level. The CDC notes that mpox has transitioned toward endemic circulation, with ongoing low-level transmission. Despite this, the survey shows some progress, such as 73% of respondents knowing that people with mpox should isolate until the rash is gone. However, knowledge gaps persist, with only 18% correctly identifying mpox as less contagious than COVID-19. Misinformation about the virus, including claims about its origin and transmission, remains prevalent, highlighting the need for improved public education.
Original source text (English)
Skip to main content Main navigation News Antimicrobial Resistance COVID-19 Chronic Wasting Disease Influenza Public Health Alerts All Topics A-Z Coronavirus Vaccines Roadmap Influenza Vaccines Roadmap Roadmaps for Priority Pathogens Vaccine Integrity Project CIDRAP Leadership Forum . . Topics & Projects Antimicrobial Stewardship Chronic Wasting Disease Osterholm Update Podcasts Newsletters About CIDRAP CIDRAP in the News Our Staff Our Director Osterholm in the Press Shop Merchandise Contact Us About Search CLOSE Search Support Menu As mpox continues to circulate, US adults’ awareness of the virus has plummeted since 2022 Laine Bergeson Mpox Jacob Wackerhausen / iStock Share Copied to clipboard Four years after a large global mpox outbreak, Americans’ awareness of the viral illness has dropped significantly, according to a survey today from the Annenberg Public Policy Center (APPC) at the University of Pennsylvania. Of the 1,842 US adults surveyed from September 22 to October 5, 2026, only 8% said they had seen, read, or heard anything about mpox in the past month. That number is down from 11% in July 2024—and down 80% in July 2022. While case counts have dropped nationwide over the past four years, the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) says that “ongoing, low-level transmission has persisted,” which indicates “a transition toward endemic circulation.” Mpox is transmitted by close contact with the scabs, rashes, or bodily fluids of an infected person. It can also be spread by contact with the clothing, bedding, towels, and dishes of an infected person. Sexual transmission is a common route. Less than half know about mpox vaccine Roughly three-quarters (76%) of respondents said they are "not too familiar" or “not at all familiar” with mpox, and just 7% said they are “somewhat” or “very worried” about contracting it in the next three months. That number is significantly lower than the percentage of people (21%) who said they were worried about contracting it in August 2022. Almost two-thirds (62%) said they were unsure about the contagiousness of mpox, and only 18% correctly said it is less contagious than COVID-19. In August 2022, 41% said they knew it was less contagious than COVID. About half (49%) of survey respondents said they know how mpox is spread, while 51% recognized that face-to-face contact with an infected person increases the risk of infection. Those numbers are relatively unchanged from 2024. As the epidemiology of mpox changes, public understanding of who is at risk, how the virus spreads, and how vaccination can protect against it needs to keep pace. Knowledge about the mpox vaccine has dropped since August 2022, when 61% of people said they were aware that an mpox vaccine exists. Last month, less than half (44%) said they knew about the existence of an mpox vaccine. Forty-eight percent were unsure whether a vaccine exists. “The persistence of these knowledge gaps matters because the virus has not stood still,” says APPC’s managing director of survey research, Ken Winneg, PhD, in an APPC news release. “As the epidemiology of mpox changes, public understanding of who is at risk, how the virus spreads, and how vaccination can protect against it needs to keep pace.” Signs of progress, persistent misinformation While some misperceptions about mpox persist—16% of respondents said that the virus was definitely or probably bioengineered in a laboratory (a claim without evidence), and 7% incorrectly said that receiving the COVID vaccine increases the risk of getting mpox—the survey showed some signs of increased knowledge about the viral illness and openness to vaccination. Nearly three quarters (73%) of respondents said they know that people with mpox should isolate until the rash is gone—an increase from 68% in 2024—and the same percentage of respondents said the benefits of being vaccinated against mpox outweigh the risks. Sixty-nine percent of people said they would be “somewhat” or “very” likely to get an mpox vaccine if they were exposed to the virus, with the proportion who said they were “very” likely increasing, from 38% in 2024 to 44% last month. The survey’s margin of error is plus or minus 3.2 percentage points. 河流连通性重组 猴痘 传播 刚果民主共和国农村地区的风险 - Bioengineer.org

River Connectivity Restructures Mpox Transmission Risk Across Rural Democratic Republic of Congo - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报2022 年至 2025 年间在刚果民主共和国马伊-恩东贝省进行的一项地理模型研究表明,河流连通性显着影响农村地区的 猴痘 传播 风险。这项研究于 2026 年 11 月 18 日发表,由 Christian M. Ibolobolo、Harry César Kayembe、Didier Bompangue 和 Dav M. Ebengo 领导,强调了人类跨越河流走廊和有限的道路基础设施的流动如何重塑病原体的扩散。研究发现,流行病模式是由不对称的连通性而非均匀的空间传播驱动的,基尼系数为0.65,表明大多数空间流动集中在关键的航行走廊沿线。研究人员使用一个综合空间框架,结合环境成本面、相互作用模型和分区疾病动态来分析不同地形上的运动摩擦。他们还应用模糊层次分析过程来计算障碍权重,并使用蒙特卡洛模拟来解决 监测 和报告中的不确定性。研究结果表明,Bokoro、Nioki 和 Mushie 应被指定为永久哨兵 监测,因为它们在 Lukenie、Fimi 和 Kasai 河流走廊中具有高度中心作用。该研究强调了了解疾病模型中的空间连通性对于有效的公共卫生干预措施的重要性。

时间
该研究于2022年至2025年间进行,发表日期为2026年11月18日(N59)。

该研究重点关注刚果民主共和国的马伊-恩东贝省,该省横跨广阔的冲积平原、水貌洼地和热带雨林,面积达 11,403.8 平方公里 (N9)。
事件
由研究人员 Christian M. Ibolobolo、Harry César Kayembe、Didier Bompangue 和 Dav M. 进行的一项地理模型研究评估了 2022 年至 2025 年间人类穿越河流走廊和受限道路系统的流动性如何重塑 猴痘 传播。
规模、影响与风险
研究表明,流行病轨迹受不对称连通性而非均匀空间传播的影响,基尼系数为 0.65,表明空间流动集中在特权走廊 (N29) 上。
应对、状态与不确定性
研究团队应用模糊层次分析法来计算障碍权重,并使用蒙特卡罗模拟来量化诊断和报告的不确定性(N17、N16)。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

River Connectivity Restructures Mpox Transmission Risk Across Rural Democratic Republic of Congo - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A geographic modeling study conducted in Maï-Ndombe province, Democratic Republic of the Congo, between 2022 and 2025, reveals that river connectivity significantly influences Mpox transmission risk in rural areas. Published on November 18, 2026, the research, led by Christian M. Ibolobolo, Harry César Kayembe, Didier Bompangue, and Dav M. Ebengo, highlights how human mobility across fluvial corridors and limited road infrastructure reshape pathogen diffusion. The study found that epidemic patterns are driven by asymmetric connectivity rather than uniform spatial spread, with a Gini coefficient of 0.65 showing that most spatial flows concentrate along key navigational corridors. The researchers used an integrated spatial framework combining environmental cost surfaces, interaction models, and compartmental disease dynamics to analyze movement friction across varied terrain. They also applied a Fuzzy Analytic Hierarchy Process to compute barrier weights and used Monte Carlo simulations to address uncertainties in surveillance and reporting. The findings suggest that Bokoro, Nioki, and Mushie should be designated as permanent sentinel surveillance posts due to their high-centrality roles in the Lukenie, Fimi, and Kasai river corridors. The study underscores the importance of understanding spatial connectivity in disease modeling for effective public health interventions.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Monday, November 23, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health River Connectivity Restructures Mpox Transmission Risk Across Rural Democratic Republic of Congo by November 18, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 6 mins read 0 River Connectivity Restructures Mpox Transmission Risk Across Rural Democratic Republic of Congo Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Integrating multimodal spatial connectivity into epidemiological forecasting models substantially improves Mpox incidence predictions within major transport hubs while degrading overall provincial-scale model fit, according to a geographic modeling study conducted in Maï-Ndombe province in the Democratic Republic of the Congo. The investigation, published in the International Journal of Health Geographics by researchers Christian M. Ibolobolo, Harry César Kayembe, Didier Bompangue, and Dav M. Ebengo, evaluated how human mobility across fluvial corridors and constrained road systems reshapes pathogen diffusion between 2022 and 2025. In vast, forested tropical landscapes where degraded overland infrastructure leaves riverways as the primary avenues of transport, understanding the spatial architecture of disease transmission remains difficult due to the scarcity of empirical travel records and fragile surveillance networks. By evaluating an explicit compartmental framework against empirical case distributions, the team demonstrated that epidemic trajectories across rural territories are fundamentally conditioned by asymmetric connectivity rather than uniform spatial spread. Maï-Ndombe province exemplifies the severe physical and infrastructural frictions that govern movement throughout the central Congo Basin. Extending across a low-relief landscape with an average elevation of 500 meters above sea level, the province spans extensive alluvial plains and hydromorphic depressions. Tropical rainforest covers approximately 11,403.8 square kilometers, representing 87 percent of the territory across Oshwe, Kiri, Inongo, and northern Kutu, with 15.93 percent of these forest systems established directly on hydromorphic soils. Conversely, the southern and southwestern sectors, including Bolobo, Mushie, Kwamouth, Yumbi, and southern Kutu, exhibit savanna environments interspersed with gallery forest fragments. Infrastructure throughout the region remains sparse, featuring an average of only one health center per health zone across territories covering thousands of square kilometers. Subsistence farming dominates the rural economy, which relies heavily on extracting timber, fish, and agricultural products destined primarily for Kinshasa, leaving inter-zone circulation heavily dependent on navigable riverways. To capture the mechanical resistance exerted by this complex landscape, the authors formulated an integrated spatial framework linking environmental cost surfaces, interaction models, and compartmental disease dynamics. The researchers addressed three critical gaps in previous epidemiological literature: the absence of combined road and river networks within Mpox connectivity modeling in rural tropical Africa, the lack of quantified intra-provincial connectivity matrices in Maï-Ndombe, and the absence of direct comparative evaluations between metapopulation models operating with and without spatial connectivity. All spatial, demographic, and epidemiological layers were assembled from open-access databases, reprojected into the Universal Transverse Mercator coordinate system zone 33 South, and resampled via bilinear interpolation to a metric resolution of 30 meters. Epidemiological records were corrected for underreporting using the surveillance adequacy score, while Monte Carlo simulations quantified surrounding diagnostic and reporting uncertainties. Quantifying movement friction across varied terrain required an analytical hierarchy process capable of handling expert uncertainty without on-the-ground travel surveys. The research team applied a Fuzzy Analytic Hierarchy Process to compute barrier weights across environmental criteria, representing qualitative pairwise comparisons using triangular fuzzy numbers and Chang’s defuzzification method. Friction coefficients were established for forest, open water, forest mosaic, hydromorphic zones, savanna, and topographic slope. Dense forest and water bodies emerged as dominant friction barriers, each receiving normalized weights of 0.31, followed by hydromorphic zones at 0.15, slope at 0.12, forest mosaics at 0.091, and savanna landscapes at 0.053. Travel speeds were categorized separately across transport modes, establishing velocities between 30 and 50 kilometers per hour along road paths and 5 to 10 kilometers per hour along navigable waterways, allowing a least-cost path algorithm to trace minimum friction paths between health zones. The resultant distance-cost matrices were incorporated into an enhanced gravity model designed to approximate inter-zone interaction volumes. Traditional Newtonian gravity formulations scale interaction proportional to origin and destination populations divided by squared Euclidean separation; the researchers enhanced this by substituting environmental friction costs and exponential deterrence functions. Health areas with populations below 50,000 residents were classified as origin zones, while zones exceeding 50,000 residents served as destination attractors. To simulate disease transmission, the computed connectivity flows were introduced into a spatialized metapopulation Susceptible-Exposed-Infected-Recovered system. The infection force was partitioned between an endogenous local transmission component representing contacts within individual health zones and an exogenous connectivity-driven component driven by incoming inter-zone interactions. Baseline parameters established an initial transmission rate of 0.4695, a progression rate of 0.8703 per week representing a 9- to 10-day incubation interval, and a recovery rate of 0.8023 per week, evaluated alongside a sensitivity weighting parameter bounded between 0.6 and 0.8. Structural analysis of the resulting mobility network confirmed extreme spatial concentration and inequality across provincial transit axes. Calculating network dispersion revealed a Gini coefficient of 0.65, demonstrating that the vast majority of spatial flows are concentrated along a small number of privileged navigational corridors. This systemic concentration was corroborated by a high coefficient of variation of 1.52 and a Shannon entropy value of 4.43, demonstrating that mobility in Maï-Ndombe is not dispersed randomly but instead organized along dominant structural axes. The primary interaction corridor follows the Lukenie, Fimi, and Kasai rivers, funneling regional movements directly through central river ports. Gravitational closeness centrality calculations confirmed this hierarchy, placing Bokoro at the operational center of the province with the highest centrality value of 0.00147, followed by Mushie at 0.00121, Yumbi at 0.00085, and Inongo at 0.000843, while peripheral zones such as Mimia, Oshwe, and Kiri recorded near-zero centrality scores. When these connectivity flows were evaluated against empirical case records from 2022, the aggregate provincial performance of the spatialized model exhibited an unexpected deterioration. The baseline SEIR formulation omitting connectivity yielded a root mean square error of 30.26 and a mean absolute error of 19.44 cases, whereas incorporating connectivity degraded the provincial fit, producing an RMSE of 44.38, an MAE of 30.12, and a negative relative precision change of minus 0.468. This aggregate loss in predictive accuracy demonstrates that, across the entire provincial expanse, epidemic dynamics are predominantly governed by endogenous local transmission chains rather than continuous inter-zone diffusion. The broad degradation reflects known operational limitations, including surveillance notification delays, systematic underreporting linked to geographic remoteness, and structural limitations within the gravity model formulation itself. Despite aggregate performance deterioration at the provincial level, zone-by-zone precision gain analyses revealed profound local predictive improvements within structurally connected transit hubs. In health zones situated along major navigable waterways, incorporating spatial interaction dramatically enhanced model accuracy: predictive precision improved by 59.8 percent in Bokoro, 33.3 percent in Mushie, and 17.3 percent in Nioki. These sharp improvements confirm that inter-zone mobility flows constitute an authentic and measurable driver of Mpox introduction and amplification within core river settlements. By contrast, structurally isolated peripheral zones exhibited substantial reductions in model fit, with predictive performance dropping by 46.1 percent in Kiri, 27.4 percent in Mimia, 22.4 percent in Oshwe, and 11.8 percent in Pendjua, where the gravity formulation overestimates exogenous exposure in areas dominated almost entirely by isolated local transmission. The authors highlighted several structural mechanisms to explain why the inclusion of connectivity produces this divergent pattern between central hubs and isolated peripheries. A critical factor is the gravity model’s reliance on static residential population counts as denominators within the exogenous force of infection. This static denominator fails to account for temporal fluctuations in the effective population physically present in each zone during seasonal travel periods, causing models to overestimate imported infection risk in remote locations. Furthermore, the linear partitioning of transmission rates through fixed sensitivity coefficients cannot fully capture non-linear threshold phenomena or the brief duration of contact that travelers experience at river destinations before returning home. In asymmetric hub-and-spoke networks, multi-directional flow exchanges can also dilute localized exposure, generating model discrepancies when return flows are omitted from static travel matrices. These analytical findings offer direct operational guidance for designing targeted public health interventions and surveillance architectures in resource-constrained tropical regions. Identifying Bokoro, Nioki, and Mushie as high-centrality relay nodes along the Lukenie, Fimi, and Kasai river corridors indicates that these hubs should be designated as permanent sentinel surveillance posts. Health authorities can maximize early outbreak detection by stationing mobile surveillance teams and pre-positioning diagnostic stockpiles at key river embarkation points during peak traffic periods and major market gatherings. Conversely, because conventional surveillance pipelines consistently fail to capture silent transmission across remote territories like Mimia, Oshwe, and Kiri, public health planners must prioritize decentralized community-based surveillance rather than centralized reporting systems across peripheral zones. To overcome current analytical constraints, the investigators outlined three clear methodological priorities for future epidemiological research in data-scarce environments. First, static gravity formulations must evolve into dynamic spatiotemporal metapopulation frameworks that explicitly parameterize destination residence times, capturing the precise proportion of time peripheral residents spend interacting within high-density river hubs. Second, the linear partitioning of transmission parameters should be replaced by spectral analyses of next-generation transmission matrices to mathematically determine exact invasion thresholds and vulnerability metrics without relying on arbitrary weighting constants. Finally, implementing field-collected travel diaries at primary river ports will provide empirical mobility parameters, reducing reliance on theoretical cost-distance calibrations and strengthening outbreak preparedness across the forested basins of Central Africa. 在高级 HIV 尸检中发现具有复制能力的 猴痘病毒 和耐药性 - Bioengineer.org

Replication-Competent Mpox Virus and Drug Resistance Found in Advanced HIV Autopsy - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报Bioengineer.org 于 2026 年 11 月 18 日发表的一项研究报告称,在一名晚期 HIV-1 已故患者的中枢神经系统 (CNS) 中发现了具有复制能力的 猴痘病毒 (MPXV) 和耐药性。尸检分析了来自不同解剖部位的 76 个样本,证实所有测试的中枢神经系统组织中都存在 MPXV。基因组测序揭示了广泛存在的 特考韦瑞 耐药相关变异,凸显了尽管抗逆转录病毒治疗仍存在持续病毒感染的可能性。研究结果表明 MPXV 可以在中枢神经系统中积极复制,并且可能对当前的 治疗 具有抵抗力,为未来的治疗开发提供了重要的见解。本案例研究没有报告任何公共卫生反应或更广泛的 暴发 数据,而是重点关注病毒在 HIV 阳性个体的中枢神经系统中存在的生物学影响。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究发表于 2026 年 11 月 18 日,证据中没有明确说明事件日期。
地点与对象
该案例研究涉及一名晚期 HIV-1 死亡患者,但没有提供具体的地理或人口详细信息。
事件
研究人员在一名已故晚期 HIV-1 患者的中枢神经系统 (CNS) 中发现了具有复制能力的 特考韦瑞 (MPXV),并证实了 特考韦瑞 耐药变异体的存在。
规模、影响与风险
该研究集中于单个病例,没有报告病例或死亡人数。中枢神经系统中具有复制能力的 MPXV 和耐药性的存在表明未来治疗策略的潜在风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究通过广泛的尸检进行,分析了 76 个样本。这些发现基于基因组测序和原位杂交,没有提及正在进行的公共卫生反应或进一步调查。

Replication-Competent Mpox Virus and Drug Resistance Found in Advanced HIV Autopsy - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A study published on November 18, 2026, by Bioengineer.org reports the discovery of replication-competent mpox virus (MPXV) and drug resistance in the central nervous system (CNS) of a deceased patient with advanced HIV-1. The autopsy, which analyzed 76 samples from various anatomical sites, confirmed the presence of MPXV in all tested CNS tissues. Genomic sequencing revealed widespread tecovirimat resistance-associated variants, highlighting the potential for persistent viral infection despite antiretroviral therapy. The findings suggest that MPXV can actively replicate in the CNS and may be resistant to current treatments, providing critical insights for future therapeutic development. This case study does not report any public health response or broader outbreak data, focusing instead on the biological implications of the virus's presence in the CNS of an HIV-positive individual.

HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Monday, November 23, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Replication-Competent Mpox Virus and Drug Resistance Found in Advanced HIV Autopsy by November 18, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 2 mins read 0 Replication-Competent Mpox Virus and Drug Resistance Found in Advanced HIV Autopsy Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Researchers identified replication-competent mpox virus (MPXV) within the central nervous system (CNS) of a deceased patient who suffered from advanced human immunodeficiency virus (HIV-1). The autopsy study was conducted on a fatal case of mpox following the global MPXV clade IIb outbreak of more than 100,000 cases. The patient had achieved viral suppression of HIV but continued to exhibit poor immune reconstitution despite more than six months of antiretroviral therapy. The clinical course was marked by progressive necrotizing mucocutaneous mpox disease that persisted despite the administration of multiple prolonged mpox-targeted therapies. To investigate the scope of the infection, the study team conducted an extensive autopsy, quantifying MPXV DNA across 76 samples collected from diverse anatomical sites throughout the body. While the highest viral burden was identified in skin and mucosal tissues, the analysis also confirmed the presence of the virus in all central nervous system tissues tested. Crucially, the researchers detected replication-competent MPXV in multiple CNS tissues, providing evidence that the virus can actively persist in these regions. Using RNA-scope in situ hybridization, the team confirmed that both neuronal and glial cells were infected by the virus. By demonstrating that replication-competent MPXV and tecovirimat-resistant isolates can both infect and persist in the CNS, this case study provides essential data for future therapeutic design. 柬埔寨记录 30 个月内首例 猴痘 病例

Cambodia Records First Mpox Case in 30 Months - Cambodianess
新闻简报柬埔寨于2026年11月22日报道,确认了30个月零六天内的首例猴痘病例。该病例在金边发现,卫生部强调需要保持警惕和采取预防措施,以避免进一步传播。猴痘 是一种由通过密切身体接触传播的病毒引起的传染病,对于免疫系统较弱的人、孕妇、儿童和艾滋病毒感染者来说可能是致命的。该部已发布指南,敦促人们进行安全的性接触,避免与感染者直接接触,并保持卫生。该病例是孤立病例,没有其他病例或死亡报告。响应包括公共警报和限制 传播 的预防措施。
查看新闻五要素
时间
30个月内首例猴痘病例于2026年11月22日确诊。事件日期没有明确说明,但发布日期为2026年11月22日。
地点与对象
该病例是在柬埔寨,特别是金边报道的。受影响人群包括可能接触过该病毒的人,特别是免疫系统较弱的人、孕妇和儿童。
事件
柬埔寨确诊 30 个月零 6 天以来首例 猴痘 病例。卫生部报告了该病例,并强调需要保持警惕并采取预防措施,以避免进一步传播。
规模、影响与风险
该病例是孤立病例,没有提及其他病例或死亡。据世界卫生组织称,猴痘 对于免疫系统较弱的人、孕妇、儿童和艾滋病毒感染者来说可能是致命的。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生部已发布预防措施,包括安全性行为、避免与感染者接触以及保持卫生。采取这些措施是为了减轻 传播 的风险。

Cambodia Records First Mpox Case in 30 Months - Cambodianess

News Brief
Cambodia has confirmed its first mpox case in 30 months and six days, reported on November 22, 2026. The case was identified in Phnom Penh, and the Ministry of Health emphasized the need for vigilance and prevention measures to avoid further spread. Mpox, an infectious disease caused by a virus transmitted through close physical contact, can be deadly for individuals with weak immune systems, pregnant women, children, and those living with HIV. The Ministry has issued guidelines urging people to practice safe sexual contact, avoid direct contact with infected individuals, and maintain hygiene. The case is isolated, with no additional cases or deaths reported. The response includes public alerts and preventive actions to limit transmission.
Original source text (English)
Health Cambodia Records First Mpox Case in 30 Months FILE PHOTO: A nurse stands next to a newly created mpox isolation ward at a civil hospital in Ahmedabad on September 10, 2024. Formerly known as monkeypox, mpox is an infectious disease caused by a virus transmitted to humans by infected animals that can also be passed from human to human through close physical contact. (Photo by Sam PANTHAKY / AFP) By: Meng Seavmey November 22, 2026 , 12:28 PM 00:00 / 01:26 PHNOM PENH – Cambodia has recorded the first infection of Mpox, or monkeypox, case in 30 months and six days. The last one had appeared in 2024. The Ministry of Health on Nov. 22 confirmed the new case, reporting that the patient is under medical care at a hospital. Mpox is an infectious disease that can be deadly for people with weak immune systems. It causes several painful symptoms such as rash, enlarged lymph nodes, fever, headache, muscle ache, back pain and low energy. Cambodia had confirmed 20 cases of Mpox between Dec. 12, 2023, and May 14, 2024. Patients with Mpox can recover within two to four weeks. The Ministry of Health has issued an alert, telling the public to increase vigilance regarding their health and that of their families to prevent infection. The virus is transmitted from person to person through all forms of sexual contact and through direct contact with wounds, body fluids, saliva and equipment contaminated with the virus. If a pregnant woman has the virus, it can be transmitted to her child through the placenta during or after birth, according to the World Health Organization. The complications from mpox can be deadly and cause a higher risk of serious illness and death for children, pregnant women and people with weak immune systems, which also includes people living with HIV. The Ministry of Health has issued some prevention measures for people to take. They include: having safe sexual contact with protection; avoiding multiple sexual partners; avoiding direct contact with Mpox patients; avoiding contact with clothes, mattresses or pillows, towels, and dining materials used by Mpox patients; and avoiding contact with body fluids or saliva of Mpox patients. People, especially individuals who look after Mpox patients, are urged to practice regular daily sanitary measures, especially washing their hands with soap and alcohol gels. People who believe they might be experiencing the mentioned symptoms as well individuals diagnosed with the infection must remain away from people with no Mpox, and this, until all scabs fall from their skin and until they are tested negative for the virus. Original title (English)
Cambodia reports first mpox case in two and a half years - 联合早报
News Brief
Cambodia has confirmed its first mpox case in two and a half years, reported on 22 November 2026. The patient was diagnosed on 20 November and is receiving treatment in a hospital. This marks the first new case after 30 months and six days without any reported mpox cases. The Cambodian health ministry urged the public to remain vigilant and take precautions to prevent clusters or large-scale outbreaks. They advised individuals experiencing symptoms such as a blistering rash and fever to seek medical advice or get tested. The statement provided no further details about the patient's origin or transmission route. The case was confirmed through official health ministry statements, and the response includes public health reminders and prevention guidance.

(Illustrative image) Cambodia has confirmed a new mpox case, its first in two and a half years. The Cambodian health ministry said in a statement on Sunday (22 November) that the patient was diagnosed with mpox on Friday (20 November) and is receiving treatment in hospital, Xinhua reported. “This is the first new case detected in Cambodia after 30 months and six days without any reported mpox cases,” the ministry said. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

柬埔寨报告 30 多个月以来首例 猴痘 病例

Cambodia reports first mpox case in more than 30 months - سانا
新闻简报柬埔寨卫生部于 2026 年 11 月 22 日报告了该国 30 多个月以来首例 猴痘 病例。该病例涉及刚果民主共和国戈马附近 Munigi 的 治疗 中心的一名疑似感染 猴痘 的儿童。卫生部通过 X 的声明证实了这一病例,并敦促公众采取预防措施,以避免进一步传播。这种疾病是一种与天花有关的病毒感染,通过密切接触或受污染的物体传播。症状包括发烧、淋巴结肿大和皮疹,通常在两到四周内消退。卫生部建议避免与感染者密切接触,不共用个人物品,并定期进行手部消毒。鼓励任何出现症状的人立即就医。没有提供更多病例或死亡人数,应对措施仍然集中在遏制和公共卫生指导上。
查看新闻五要素
时间
柬埔寨首例 猴痘 病例于 2026 年 11 月 22 日报告,事件日期与发布日期相同。
地点与对象
该病例是在柬埔寨报道的,具体涉及刚果民主共和国戈马附近穆尼吉的 猴痘 中心的一名疑似感染 猴痘 的儿童。
事件
根据该部在 X 上发布的声明,柬埔寨卫生部宣布了该国 30 多个月以来首例 猴痘 病例。
规模、影响与风险
这是柬埔寨 30 多个月以来首次报告病例,没有提供更多病例或死亡人数。该部强调需要采取预防措施,以减轻进一步传播的风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
该部已发布预防指南,包括避免与感染者密切接触、不使用个人物品以及定期进行手部消毒。案件已确认,回应正在进行中。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Cambodia reports first mpox case in more than 30 months - سانا

News Brief
Cambodia's Ministry of Health reported the first mpox case in the country in over 30 months on November 22, 2026. The case involved a child suspected of having mpox at a treatment center in Munigi, near Goma, Democratic Republic of the Congo. The ministry confirmed the case through a statement on X and urged the public to follow preventive measures to avoid further spread. The disease, which is a viral infection related to smallpox, spreads through close contact or contaminated objects. Symptoms include fever, swollen lymph nodes, and a skin rash, typically resolving within two to four weeks. The ministry advised avoiding close contact with infected individuals, not sharing personal belongings, and regular hand disinfection. Anyone experiencing symptoms is encouraged to seek medical attention promptly. No additional case or death counts were provided, and the response remains focused on containment and public health guidance.
Original source text (English)

File photo: Reuters Phnom Penh, Nov. 22 (SANA) Cambodia’s Ministry of Health announced on Sunday the country’s first mpox case in more than two and a half years, urging the public to follow preventive measures. In a statement posted on X on Sunday, the ministry said the case was the first reported in Cambodia in more than 30 months. The country had previously recorded 20 cases of the disease. The ministry urged people to avoid close contact with infected individuals and not to use their personal belongings. It advised regular hand disinfection and called on anyone experiencing symptoms, including skin rashes, fever or muscle pain, to seek medical attention promptly. Mpox is a viral disease belonging to the same family as smallpox. It spreads between people mainly through close contact or contact with contaminated objects. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

宾夕法尼亚大学调查:猴痘病例在美国持续存在,但公众意识较低 - Audacy

Penn survey: Monkeypox cases persist in US, but public awareness is low - Audacy
新闻简报宾夕法尼亚大学安纳伯格公共政策中心于 2026 年 10 月完成的一项调查发现,尽管此类案例持续存在,但美国公众对 猴痘 的认知度仍然较低。调查指出,只有不到一半的受访者知道疫苗的存在,只有33%的人知道男男性行为者感染的风险更高。尽管 CDC 已报告至少 31 个州和波多黎各发生 200 多例 I 型 猴痘 病例,但该病毒不是通过空气传播,而是通过密切的皮肤接触传播。该研究还强调了一些知识的增长,例如 30% 的受访者知道 猴痘 可以通过孕妇的胎盘传播,这一比例高于 2024 年 7 月的 21%。该调查没有提供经证实的 猴痘 数据,但强调了对 猴痘 认识和预防的持续公共卫生担忧。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该调查于2026年10月结束,新闻于2026年11月21日发布。研究指出,自全球猴痘事件以来,猴痘案件在美国已持续存在四年,并且近年来全球70多个国家都出现了暴发事件。2024 年,由于一种新形式的 猴痘 导致症状较轻且更容易传播,病例激增。
地点与对象
该调查是在美国,特别是在费城进行的,涉及普通民众。截至 2026 年 10 月,美国疾病控制与预防中心 (CDC) 报告称,至少 31 个州和波多黎各出现了 200 多例 I 型猴痘病例。
事件
宾夕法尼亚大学安纳伯格公共政策中心进行的一项调查发现,公众对 猴痘 的认知度仍然较低,只有不到一半的受访者知道 疫苗 的存在。调查还指出,33%的受访者意识到男男性行为者感染猴痘的风险较高,并且有一些迹象表明人们对该病毒的了解有所增加,例如30%的受访者知道该病毒可以通过孕妇的胎盘传播。
规模、影响与风险
猴痘 病例持续存在于美国,并已在全球 70 多个国家/地区报告。CDC 报告了至少 31 个州和波多黎各的 200 多例 I 型病例。该病毒通过密切的皮肤接触传播,可能导致面部、手、胸部和生殖器损伤。该研究强调,该病毒不是通过空气传播的,大多数病例发生在布隆迪、乌干达和刚果民主共和国。
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 建议高危人群服用两剂 JYNNEOS 疫苗。该调查表明,人们的认识有所提高,例如 73% 的人知道 猴痘 患者应该在家隔离直到皮疹消失,这一比例高于 2024 年 7 月的 21%。该研究是一项调查,并未提供经证实的 暴发 数据,但它强调了持续存在的公共卫生问题。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Penn survey: Monkeypox cases persist in US, but public awareness is low - Audacy

News Brief
A survey by the Annenberg Public Policy Center of the University of Pennsylvania, concluded in October 2026, found that public awareness of mpox remains low in the United States, despite persistent cases. The survey noted that less than half of respondents are aware of the existence of a vaccine, and only 33% know that men who have sex with men are at higher risk of infection. While the CDC has reported over 200 clade I mpox cases in at least 31 states and Puerto Rico, the virus is not airborne and spreads through close, skin-to-skin contact. The study also highlights some increased knowledge, such as 30% of respondents knowing that mpox can spread through the placenta of a pregnant person, up from 21% in July 2024. The survey does not provide confirmed outbreak data but underscores ongoing public health concerns regarding mpox awareness and prevention.
