John has won... See Full Bio Follow: The Associated Press The Associated Press See Full Bio Follow: Nov 21, 2026, 04:01 PM ET LocalNewskywnewsradio PHILADELPHIA (KYW Newsradio) — It’s been four years since the global mpox virus outbreak. A new survey found that even though cases persist in the United States, public awareness remains low. The Annenberg Public Policy Center of the University of Pennsylvania conducted an mpox, also known as monkeypox, survey that concluded in October. It found that familiarity with the virus remains low, few worry about contracting it, and knowledge about the vaccine has not improved, with less than half not knowing a vaccine even exists. About 33% know that men who have sex with men are at higher risk of mpox infection. Mpox was first identified by scientists in 1958 when monkeys had an outbreak of a “pox-like” disease. Until about a few years ago, it was mainly seen in people in central and West Africa who had close contact with infected animals. There are two types: clade I and clade II. In 2022, the virus was confirmed to spread through sex. According to the Associated Press, there were outbreaks in more than 70 countries across the world that had not previously reported mpox. Those cases trace mainly to clade II, according to the CDC. Two years later, in 2024, cases jumped globally because of a new form of mpox that caused milder symptoms and lesions on the genitals, making it harder to detect and easier to spread. The vast majority of cases were in Burundi, Uganda, and the Democratic Republic of the Congo. Mpox belongs to the same family of viruses as smallpox but causes milder symptoms like fever, chills and body aches. People with more serious cases can develop lesions on the face, hands, chest and genitals. It’s not an airborne disease and typically requires close, skin-to-skin contact to spread. The two-dose JYNNEOS vaccine is recommended for those at increased risk of monkeypox. As of October, the CDC has reported more than 200 clade I monkeypox cases in at least 31 states and Puerto Rico. According to the study, there are some signs of increased knowledge about the virus. About 30% are aware it can spread through the placenta of a pregnant person who has mpox, an increase from 21% in July 2024. About 73% know that people with mpox should isolate at home until the rash is gone. That’s up 68% from two years ago. mpox monkeypox Around the Web Costco Shoppers Are Going Nuts for These Hearing Aids Nebroo Hearing Aids I Thought It Was a Regular Mug Until I Added Hot Water Peoasis Here Are 27 of The Coolest Gifts for This 2026 topchicdeals Looking for a Unique Gift? The survey was concluded in October 2026, with the news published on November 21, 2026. The study notes that mpox cases have persisted in the US for four years since the global outbreak, and there were outbreaks in more than 70 countries globally in recent years. In 2024, cases jumped due to a new form of mpox that caused milder symptoms and easier spread.
Location and population
The survey was conducted in the United States, specifically in Philadelphia, and involved the general population. The CDC reported more than 200 clade I monkeypox cases in at least 31 states and Puerto Rico as of October 2026.
Event
A survey conducted by the Annenberg Public Policy Center of the University of Pennsylvania found that public awareness of mpox remains low, with less than half of respondents knowing a vaccine exists. The survey also noted that 33% of respondents are aware that men who have sex with men are at higher risk of mpox infection, and there are some signs of increased knowledge about the virus, such as 30% knowing it can spread through the placenta of a pregnant person.
Scale, impact and risk
Mpox cases persist in the US and have been reported in more than 70 countries globally. The CDC reported over 200 clade I cases in at least 31 states and Puerto Rico. The virus spreads through close, skin-to-skin contact and can cause lesions on the face, hands, chest, and genitals. The study highlights that the virus is not airborne and that the majority of cases are in Burundi, Uganda, and the Democratic Republic of the Congo.
Response, status and uncertainty
The CDC recommends the two-dose JYNNEOS vaccine for those at increased risk. The survey indicates some increased awareness, such as 73% knowing that people with mpox should isolate at home until the rash is gone, up from 21% in July 2024. The study is a survey and does not provide confirmed outbreak data, but it highlights ongoing public health concerns.

图尔库日托中心发现 猴痘 病例 - Yle

Mpox case detected at a Turku daycare centre - Yle
新闻简报2026 年 11 月 19 日,芬兰图尔库的一家日托中心发现了一例 猴痘(以前称为猴痘)。该市于 2026 年 11 月 17 日星期四通过儿童和青少年服务负责人 Anu Parantainen 确认了该病例。日托中心继续正常运营,该市已启动对可能接触者的接触者追踪。这是图尔库日托中心的第一例 猴痘 病例。这种疾病通常被认为并不危险,通常会在几周内消失。卫生机构建议保持良好的手部卫生和咳嗽礼仪,以降低 传播 风险。据健康与福利研究所(THL)称,今年芬兰已有12例猴痘确诊病例,超过了近年来的数字。上一次感染高峰是在 2022 年,报告了 40 多例病例。
查看新闻五要素
时间
2026 年 11 月 19 日,图尔库的一家日托中心发现了一例 猴痘 病例。该市于 2026 年 11 月 17 日星期四确认了该病例,日托中心的运营仍在正常进行。
地点与对象
该病例是在芬兰图尔库的一个日托中心发现的。该市尚未透露哪个具体设施受到影响,也没有透露感染者是儿童还是工作人员。
事件
芬兰图尔库的一家日托中心确诊了一例 猴痘 病例。该市儿童和青少年服务负责人于 2026 年 11 月 17 日星期四向 Yle 确认了此案。
规模、影响与风险
这是 猴痘 首次在图尔库的日托中心被发现。这种疾病不属于一般危险疾病,通常会在几周内自行消退。该市已联系那些可能接触该传染病的人。
应对、状态与不确定性
该市尚未透露有关受影响设施或感染者的具体细节。应对措施包括接触者追踪以及与潜在暴露者的沟通。卫生机构建议保持良好的手部卫生和咳嗽礼仪,以降低 传播 风险。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Mpox case detected at a Turku daycare centre - Yle

News Brief
A case of mpox, previously known as monkeypox, was detected at a daycare centre in Turku, Finland, on 19 November 2026. The city confirmed the case on Thursday, 17 November 2026, through its child and youth services chief, Anu Parantainen. The daycare centre continues to operate normally, and the city has initiated contact tracing for those potentially exposed. This marks the first mpox case at a daycare in Turku. The disease, which is not generally considered dangerous, typically resolves within a few weeks. The health agency recommends good hand hygiene and cough etiquette to reduce transmission risk. According to the Institute for Health and Welfare (THL), there have been 12 confirmed mpox cases in Finland this year, surpassing recent years' numbers. The previous peak in infections was in 2022, with over 40 cases reported.

Open image viewer File photo. Image: Jaana Polamo / Yle Yle News 19.11. 12:18 A case of mpox, previously called monkeypox, has been detected at a daycare centre in Turku. The southwestern city's child and youth services chief Anu Parantainen confirmed the matter to Yle on Thursday, saying she learned about the case the previous day. Operations at the daycare centre in question are continuing normally, the city said. According to Turku's Service Area Director Vesa Kulmala, the city has contacted those potentially exposed to the infectious illness. The city has not disclosed which daycare facility was affected or whether the infected individual was a child or a staff member. Mpox is a viral illness that spread from the animal kingdom to humans. Symptoms include a skin rash that progresses from blisters to crusted lesions, along with fever, headache and swollen lymph nodes. Specialist: Mpox not generally dangerous According to the City of Turku, this was the first time mpox has been detected at a daycare centre in the city. Chief infectious disease specialist Mari Kanerva from Turku University Hospital said that until now, the disease had not been detected in the Southwest Finland welfare region. Kanerva said mpox is not classified as a generally dangerous infectious disease. According to the Institute for Health and Welfare (THL), mpox can be transmitted from person to person through skin or mucous membrane contact, sexual activity, or through droplets from prolonged face-to-face contact. It can also be transmitted via shared towels or bed linen. The disease often resolves on its own within a few weeks, according to the health agency. Good hand hygiene and cough etiquette reduce the risk of transmission. So far this year, there have been 12 confirmed cases of mpox detected in Finland, according to THL — more than in recent years. The previous peak in infections was in 2022, when more than 40 cases were detected. GeoVax Labs (NASDAQ:GOVX) Gains 18% on Heavy Volume as Mpox Vaccine Program Advances - Kalkine
新闻简报GeoVax Labs(纳斯达克:GOVX)在2026年11月16日其股价上涨了18.48%,交易量为7.17万股,表明投资者兴趣有所增强。这一交易量的激增与公司其猴痘 疫苗项目,特别是针对猴痘和天花的GEO-MVA候选疫苗的进展有关。公司计划进行一项Phase 3免疫桥接研究,该研究将比较免疫应答与已有的产品,时间安排在2026年第四季度或第二季度。疫苗被定位为生物防御和公共卫生储备的潜在补充。然而,该项目的进展取决于监管反馈、资金以及研究结果。目前的证据并未提供与猴痘相关的具体病例数、死亡数或暴露数量,但强调了公司致力于开发疫苗用于猴痘和天花。股价波动反映了市场情绪和公司发展进度,而非已确认的暴发数据。公司总部位于佐治亚州亚特兰大,同时专注于疫苗和肿瘤学领域,其中Gedeptin是一种针对实体瘤的基因导向酶前药疗法。目前的证据未提及任何已确认的暴发或具体健康影响,但概述了公司对疫苗的开发及其在公共卫生准备中的潜在作用。

News Brief
GeoVax Labs (NASDAQ:GOVX) saw its stock price rise by 18.48% on 16 November 2026, with a trading volume of 7.17 million shares, indicating heightened investor interest. This surge in activity is linked to the company's advancement in its Mpox vaccine program, specifically the GEO-MVA candidate, which targets both mpox and smallpox. The company plans a Phase 3 immuno-bridging study, which compares immune responses with an established product, with timing outlined for the fourth quarter of 2026 or the second half of 2026. The vaccine is positioned as a potential addition to biodefence and public-health stockpiles. However, the progress of the program depends on regulatory feedback, funding, and the outcome of the study. The evidence does not provide specific case counts, death counts, or exposure numbers related to mpox, but it highlights the company's focus on developing vaccines for mpox and smallpox. The stock price movement reflects market sentiment and the company's development progress, rather than confirmed outbreak data. The company is based in Atlanta, Georgia, and has a dual focus on vaccines and oncology, with Gedeptin being a gene-directed enzyme prodrug therapy for solid tumours. The evidence does not mention any confirmed outbreaks or specific health impacts, but it outlines the company's vaccine development and its potential role in public health preparedness.

The company has planned a Phase 3 immuno-bridging study, a trial design that compares immune responses with an established product, with timing outlined for the fourth quarter of 2026 or the second half of 2026 depending on the report. The program is positioned as a potential addition to biodefence and public-health stockpiles. Progress depends on regulatory feedback, funding and the outcome of the planned study. Mpox is a viral disease that spread internationally in recent years, and smallpox vaccines are held in government stockpiles for preparedness. Developers of such products often depend on government contracts and regulatory pathways that differ from conventional consumer vaccines. Gedeptin and Other Platforms Beyond infectious disease, GeoVax is developing Gedeptin, a gene-directed enzyme prodrug therapy for solid tumours. The dual focus on vaccines and oncology gives the company two separate development paths. From 19 to 22 October 2026, GeoVax presented at World Vaccine Congress Europe, where it discussed its portfolio with industry participants. Gene-directed enzyme prodrug therapy delivers a gene into tumour cells so that a harmless drug is converted into a cancer-killing agent inside the tumour, an approach intended to limit exposure of healthy tissue. Funding, Risks and What to Watch Clinical-stage companies rely on Capital Markets to fund trials, and GeoVax has used private placements and warrants in the past to raise money. Further financing can dilute existing holders. Investors may follow regulatory discussions on the Phase 3 design, any government or partner funding for GEO-MVA and updates on Gedeptin. Biotech share prices can move sharply on trial news, funding events and sector sentiment. As a clinical-stage company without approved products, GeoVax's valuation reflects expectations for its pipeline rather than current sales. FAQs Q: How did GeoVax shares perform on 16 November 2026? A: The stock closed at US$2.02, up 18.48%, on volume of 7.17 million shares. Q: What is GEO-MVA? A: GEO-MVA is GeoVax's vaccine candidate for mpox and smallpox, with a Phase 3 immuno-bridging study planned. Q: Where is GeoVax based? A: GeoVax Labs is headquartered in Atlanta, Georgia. Original title (Chinese)
Nature | 痘病毒内核上的神秘通道:李赛实验室破解“分子门户”的身份与结构之谜
News Brief
The supplied evidence does not clearly report the event time in verified English. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the location or affected population in verified English. The supplied evidence does not clearly report the central event in verified English. Cell Discov 10, 108 (2024). Original source text (Chinese)
新闻 体育 汽车 房产 旅游 教育 时尚 科技 财经 娱乐 Nature | 痘病毒内核上的神秘通道:李赛实验室破解“分子门户”的身份与结构之谜 2026-11-21 17:27 来源: 城市线上生活 发布于:山西省 痘病毒是一种古老的,直径达200-360 nm的巨型囊膜病毒。作为庞大双链DNA病毒家族的成员,正痘病毒属包含天花病毒、猴痘病毒,及被广泛用作疫苗载体的痘苗病毒。尽管世界卫生组织(WHO)于1980年宣布天花已被彻底根除,但正痘病毒对人类的威胁并未消失。近年猴痘病毒在全球持续流行,至2026年7月,全球共计145个国家/地区报告确诊逾19万例猴痘感染病例,致500多例患者死亡。这些疫情警示,正痘病毒对人类健康构成持续威胁。 11月16日,《自然》(Nature)杂志以“加速预览”形式发表清华大学生命学院李赛团队题为“嵌在痘病毒内核上的门户复合物的结构 (Architecture of a portal complex embedded in the poxvirus core)”的学术文章。 在冷冻电镜时代,科学家尝试对痘病毒进行高分辨成像和原位结构解析,这面临三项艰巨挑战:1)这种形似砖头的病毒尺寸为200-360 nm,为电子穿透和拍照效率带来极大挑战(图1);2)病毒形态多样,无法通过平均整颗病毒粒子得到结构;3)它编码200种蛋白,大部分的结构和功能未知,难以进行建模。 图1:痘病毒与李赛实验室研究的其他囊膜病毒的尺度比较 与在宿主细胞核内转录的DNA病毒不同,痘病毒自带一座由致密栅栏蛋白包裹、形似装甲车的“转录工厂”,在细胞质中完成转录。然而,这种密闭的内核结构缺乏足以让较大生物分子自由穿过的间隙(图2a)。这便引出一个困扰多年的问题:这座“装甲工厂”,怎样与外界进行物质交换? 为回答这个问题,李赛实验室于2024年利用冷冻电子断层成像(cryo-ET)技术,对灭活猴痘病毒进行成像和结构解析时,意外发现一种“花瓣状”的大型分子复合物,猜测它可能是调控病毒早期转录的重要机器(1)。然而,受限于猴痘样品的实验局限,该复合物的分辨率仅达到~20 Å,无法判断其蛋白组分,因此研究团队转而研究痘苗病毒。 研究揭示,每个病毒内核表面平均分布着约20个门户复合物。这些结构贯穿内核壁,连接其内部与外部空间。这座直径约12 nm的“分子门户”具有精巧的六重对称结构,由多个蛋白质亚基组装形成,并通过复杂的互作网络嵌入内核壁。 图2:痘苗病毒内核的高分辨结构。含(a)tomogram slice,内核结构,(b)门户复合物和(c)孔道复合物的结构与分布 展开全文 图2:痘苗病毒内核的高分辨结构。含(a)tomogram slice,内核结构,(b)门户复合物和(c)孔道复合物的结构与分布 孔道复合物由四个六聚体环层层堆叠构成,犹如“少女的盛装”:顶部的“衣领(Collar)”、中部的“上衣(Coat)”,以及基部外侧的“裙子(Skirt)”与内侧的“衬裙(Petticoat)”。四层环环相扣:Coat嵌入内核壁、连接上下部分,Collar扣在Coat上方,Petticoat则围绕在通道最窄处(图3a)。 图3:痘苗病毒孔道复合物的蛋白身份和组装规律 通过整合早期功能学研究、冷冻电镜密度图特征及AlphaFold3结构预测,作者确定出复合物的蛋白身份:E6搭起中部Coat的骨架,E8构成顶部Collar,L3以两种构象分别形成Skirt与Petticoat(图3b)。三者按6:6:12的化学计量组装,接触面上的电荷高度互补,像榫卯一样彼此咬合,把这座“门”牢牢固定住。 进一步分析,研究人员发现孔道中央L3上的四个赖氨酸围成了一条长约2 nm、内径约16.5 Å且富含正电的管道。这一狭窄区在尺寸上恰好容许单链RNA及小分子原料的通过,却不足以容纳更大的双链DNA或细胞质DNA感受器(图4)。不仅如此,该狭窄区在正痘病毒属内高度保守,提示该结构可能在痘病毒生命活动中承担重要作用。 为探究其功能,中国疾病预防控制中心的谭文杰团队开展了病毒遗传学和细胞实验。他们发现,突变这四个赖氨酸中的任何一个均无法获得稳定的子代病毒。即使将其替换成同带正电荷的精氨酸,也无法完全恢复病毒功能。这表明,通道内这些氨基酸不仅需具备适当的电性,其精确的分子结构也可能对病毒复制至关重要。 图4: 孔道复合物的通道分析。四个赖氨酸残基(K261、K268、K270和K272)朝向通道内部排列,形成了一段特殊的“带正电荷的分子细管” 该研究不仅将此前模糊的痘病毒门户复合物推进至明确的原子模型,更证明了L3蛋白上的K261、K268、K270位点在病毒复制周期中发挥关键作用。这些在不同痘病毒中高度保守的位点,有望成为广谱抗痘病毒药物设计的全新靶点。 清华大学生命学院李赛副教授为本文通讯作者,2026届博士毕业生洪烨为本文第一作者。李赛团队博士研究生王柯人、博士后梁瑞、张俊霞(已出站)、彭程、博士生张哲源、张伟平;中国疾病预防控制中心病毒病预防控制所谭文杰教授、其团队研究生刘士元(已毕业)、黄保英研究员;北京大学定量生物学中心宋晨教授、其团队博士研究生朱泽锋(已毕业)参与研究。 感谢清华大学生命学院张强锋副教授、基础医学院丁强副教授在模型搭建和病毒学方面的建议,以及蛋白质科学技术中心冷冻电镜设施、生物计算平台和清华大学BSL-2公共平台的支持。 研究得到国家自然科学基金、教育部高教司及清华大学笃实专项基金的资助,及北京生物结构前沿研究中心、膜生物学全国重点实验室、清华-北大生命联合中心和生命科学人工智能研究中心的长期支持。 参考文献 1. Y. Hong et al., Molecular architecture of monkeypox mature virus. Cell Discov 10, 108 (2024). 2. J. Liu et al., A multimeric pore complex in the poxvirus core revealed by integrative structural analysis. Nat Commun 17, (2026). 3. T. Calcraft, M. Hernandez-Gonzalez, M. Way, P. B. Rosenthal, In situ structure of the poxvirus portal complex. Nature, (2026). 4. Z. Zhang et al., FlyTomo: a streamlined software for on-the-fly cryo-ET data processing and diagnosis. Original title (Chinese)
柬埔寨时隔两年半再现猴痘病例 患者20日确诊正住院治疗
News Brief
On November 22, 2026, the Cambodian Ministry of Health announced the first confirmed case of monkeypox in the country in approximately two and a half years. The patient, who was diagnosed on November 20, is currently hospitalized. This marks the resumption of monkeypox cases in Cambodia after a period of 30 months and 6 days without any reported cases. The ministry emphasized the importance of vigilance and urged individuals to seek medical attention if they exhibit symptoms such as blister-like rashes on the face, hands, feet, or other body parts, along with fever, swollen lymph nodes, and muscle pain. The transmission routes include sexual contact and direct contact with the patient's wounds, bodily fluids, or contaminated objects. No specific risk level or further details about the outbreak's scale or impact were provided. The response includes public health advisories but no detailed investigation or containment measures were mentioned.

柬埔寨卫生部11月22日发布声明说,该国发现一例猴痘(mpox)病例,这是柬埔寨连续30个月零6天没有病例后的首例,也是时隔约两年半再次出现猴痘。据新华社报道,这名患者于11月20日检测呈阳性,目前正在医院接受治疗。卫生部没有公布患者的其他信息。 资料图片 / Pexels(柬埔寨首都金边) 上一轮疫情20例 柬埔寨此前在2023年12月至2024年5月期间累计报告20例猴痘病例,之后一直没有新增。 卫生部在声明中呼吁民众保持警惕,保护自己和家人的健康,防止出现聚集性病例或大规模暴发。 怎么传播、有哪些症状 声明说,猴痘病毒可以通过各种形式的性接触在人与人之间传播,也可以通过直接接触患者的伤口、体液、唾液或被污染的物品传播;孕妇还可能在怀孕期间或分娩后把病毒传给胎儿。 卫生部提醒,脸部、手掌、脚底、身体、眼睛、口腔或生殖器出现水疱样皮疹,同时伴有发烧、淋巴结肿大、头痛、肌肉酸痛、腰痛和乏力等症状的人,应到卫生中心或医院就诊,或接受猴痘检测。 资料图片 / Pexels(柬埔寨金边夜景) 相关报道: 世卫:猴痘仍是公共卫生紧急事件 新西兰一天新增3例猴痘病例 都与皇后镇骄傲节有关 消息来源:新华社 编辑:忆雪 版权声明 1. 了解“疹”相,预防猴痘

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

猴痘,外号“天花小老弟”、“水痘远房表弟”,是由猴痘病毒感染所致的一种人畜共患病,猴痘病毒分为分支I型和分支II型。传染源包括猴痘患者及感染的啮齿类动物、猴和猿等灵长类动物。既往猴痘疫情主要在非洲地区流行,2022年后逐渐扩散至全球大多数国家和地区。临床表现主要有发热、皮疹、淋巴结肿大等症状。 皮疹是猴痘最常见的症状,出疹前可出现发热、头痛、乏力、淋巴结肿大等。皮疹多见于面部和四肢,也可出现在口腔、 ...

了解“疹”相,预防猴痘

猴痘,外号“天花小老弟”、“水痘远房表弟”,是由猴痘病毒感染所致的一种人畜共患病,猴痘病毒分为分支I型和分支II型。传染源包括猴痘患者及感染的啮齿类动物、猴和猿等灵长类动物。既往猴痘疫情主要在非洲地区流行,2022年后逐渐扩散至全球大多数国家和地区。临床表现主要有发热、皮疹、淋巴结肿大等症状。 皮疹是猴痘最常见的症状,出疹前可出现发热、头痛、乏力、淋巴结肿大等。皮疹多见于面部和四肢,也可出现在口腔、 ...
